## November 20, 2026 — AUTHOR CLARIFICATION: Blue Morph reversion has finite force; mundane manufacturing (LATEST)

**Approved:** When a temporary Blue Morph ends, the remaining morphed material attempts to return to its original shape, but **the reversion does not exert infinite or irresistible force**. Ordinary physical constraints, including a sufficiently strong surrounding mold, can resist that return. Consequently, **temporary Blue Morph can be used for durable mundane manufacturing without purchasing permanent spell duration**: for example, reshape scrap metal into a sword and promptly enclose it in a sufficiently durable mold before the spell expires; as the magic ends, the mold prevents full restoration and the metal remains physically deformed into the manufactured form. The resulting object is **mundane material**, not a permanent magical transformation or an exemption from matter conservation.

**Preserve existing boundaries:** The material must be eligible to Morph and obey the spell's Power, magnitude/material-area adjustments, targeting and normal casting requirements. Fundamental destruction still prevents ordinary reversion. Reversion does not undo injuries, movements, or other external consequences. **OPEN:** precise reversion-force formula versus the resistance/strength of molds, whether the material springs back partially, material integrity/work-hardening, and any detailed manufacturing times, tooling and quality calculations. Do not infer effortless perfect metalworking or an infinite-force shattering effect.

---

## November 20, 2026 — AUTHOR DECISION: Universal spell duration, buff escalation, instantaneous effects, and Blue Morph (LATEST)

**Approved rules and terminology:**
- **Permanent spells** require substantial mana and impose **Soul Strain** that the user must buy off with XP over time. When the permanent spell **increases attributes/stats**, the Soul Strain remains **permanent**, analogous to artificially increased stats from equipped items. Specific mana pricing, Soul Strain amounts, XP buy-off conversion, eligibility and exceptions are **OPEN**; do not silently assign numbers.
- **Multiple buff spells on one target have escalating MP costs:** the first applicable buff costs its ordinary MP price, the **second costs ×2**, the **third ×4**, and successive additional buffs continue doubling (fourth ×8, etc.). This is a safeguard against long-duration and permanent buff stacking. Exact definition of a qualifying buff, simultaneous versus sequential casting, recalculation after a buff expires, overlapping effects, ritual discounts, and how the multiplier applies to activation versus upkeep are **OPEN**.
- **Instantaneous** is the spell-duration/effect classification for magic whose *magical effect itself is brief*, but whose ordinary consequences on the world persist without continued magic, as with a fireball. Instantaneous does **not** mean permanent ongoing enchantment, undo damage, or waive normal action/casting requirements.
- **Reversion of temporary Blue Morph:** when the spell ends, remaining successfully morphed material is actively **morphed back into its original shape** rather than resetting the whole world. Events caused during the morph remain real; other unsupported objects, occupants, injuries and changes do not reverse. **Fundamentally destroyed material**, for example wood burned into ash, cannot unmorph back into its original form. A tree sunk into the ground can revert unharmed while an unsupported treehouse may be damaged.
- **Blue Morph accepts flesh** as a material with the **same two-area-category penalty as metal**. Morphing flesh constitutes **Transformation**, obeys its ordinary Power/targeting/resistance/delivery restrictions, and does not bypass unfinished universal Transformation rules.
- Earlier author's approved Morph directions preserved: **Animation is distinct from Morph**; a normal **1 MP single-target Morph** can reshape a handheld-scale object at ordinary legal casting range; one selected material category per casting, with minor variations included; **wood standard area, stone −1 area category, metal/flesh −2**, with **one-target floor**; matter is conserved; hostile applications that become attacks obey ordinary spell Power, Speed, attack and defense limits. Material hardness versus success continues to use lawful Power, not an extra automatic MP fee.
- **Magnitude area/cost working proposal:** Rank 0 one target **1 MP**; I adjacent targets **2 MP**; II 20-foot burst **4 MP**; III 100-foot burst **10 MP**; IV 1,000-foot burst **30 MP**; V 2-mile burst **60 MP**. These MP numbers are **provisional calibration**, not final approved universal costs. A large-area spell may take many turns to channel, to be designed under Rituals. Burst radius/placement interpretation and interaction with older merged Magnitude benchmarks await formal reconciliation.

**Author accepts provisional non-attack duration ladder for continued testing (NOT final numerical canon):**

| Duration | Proposed MP multiplier |
|---|---:|
| 1 turn | ×1 |
| 3 rounds | ×2 |
| 1 minute | ×3 |
| 10 minutes | ×5 |
| 1 hour | ×10 |
| 1 day | ×20 |
| 1 week | ×40 |
| Permanent | Special high-cost ritual plus Soul Strain |

**Design examples (not additional fixed costs):** A legendary Warlock may temporarily deform a forest to expose an ambushing army, with reversion around a minute later, using a comparatively brisk ritual. Permanently turning a forest into a grand citadel should be possible in principle but require one of the most substantial rituals available, rather than a cheap quick casting. Duration, ritual time and resources, casting Channel Limit, environmental interactions, precise permanent-object shape and Soul Strain amounts remain to be designed. These directions do not authorize free construction or permanent material creation.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Basic Manashifting shields, slow passage and barrier-breaking (LATEST)

**Status and precedence:** The author's latest explicit November 19 rulings supersede older suggestions of mandatory omnidirectional basic Warlock shields or a depleting shield-HP pool. These are approved qualitative foundations for the modular Manacraft migration; exact structure-damage procedures, arc, and outstanding spell Power formula remain open. Preserve the existing historical Warlock skill/rank/SP ledger until separately migrated.

- **Creature-anchored White shield:** A normal basic magical overshield is **directional, anchored to its target, and follows the target's torso orientation**. Knocking its bearer prone rotates its protected arc along with that torso, potentially leaving ordinary attackers an opening. It remains protective on **each eligible attack** during its duration, not a one-hit resource pool. Its normal defensive presence does **not** prevent allied attacks from leaving or entering, and allies' attacks are ignored by the shield.
- **Fast versus slow passage:** The shield is an obstacle to **fast, harmful strikes**: a rapidly swung sword meets resistance and can rebound. **Slow deliberate penetration** is physically possible: a careful foe may slowly reach a hand or sword through the field, but that slow poke does **not** become an injurious strike merely by touching flesh. Legitimate approach, bodily passage, positional control and grappling can bring attackers inside the shield; ordinary basic anchored shields **do not protect against close-range attacks within a grapple**. This does not allow arbitrarily relabeling a fast attack as a slow one; determine lawful attack effects using the ordinary combat and positioning rules. A trip or other maneuver must itself be legally delivered; the shield is not automatically bypassed because a maneuver is called a trip.
- **One target, one anchor shield:** An individual target may bear **one ordinary anchored shield** at a time. Multiple separately cast directional shields remain permissible across distinct eligible targets, with actual action, MP, duration, upkeep, and Channel Limit restrictions; do not invent a generic prohibition on multiple shield castings.
- **Timing and Power, author-selected baseline:** A basic anchored White Mana shield applies **100% of its eligible spell Power for the current turn**, against each attack within its protected arc; the **three-round** version applies **50% of eligible spell Power** against each such attack during that duration. The analogous basic **Red imbuement** adds its independently calculated spell-Power rider to **all eligible attacks** over the same one-turn 100% or three-round 50% options, never multiplies an entire physical attack. No extra attacks are generated. Existing action economy and Channel Limit still govern the casting. Larger-area and specialist Concordance improvements are not established by this entry.
- **Black counter:** An eligible Black Mana attack contacting a magical shield **subtracts its own applicable Power from the shield's remaining protective Power**, possibly unraveling it entirely. The reduced protection **stays reduced for the remainder of that casting**, rather than refilling each round. Black's previously author-selected wound-exploitation bonus is **+50% of its own base spell Power per existing bodily wound, capped at +100%**; that bodily-wound bonus does not automatically apply to a shield, which has no bodily wounds. Global additive-rider philosophy remains: independently calculated mana/spell effects do not multiply the completed combined attack.
- **Barrier wall (White and Green structure interface):** Unlike permeable creature-anchored shields, a **world-anchored structural barrier** stops physical travel from either side, blocks allied as well as hostile attacks, and is **probably transparent** (transparency is a design proposal, not locked). White walls and Green-created physical structures should share the future common structure-building/structure-damage system. **A physical attack that overcomes structural barrier Power can physically break the barrier**; it is not indestructible or exclusively vulnerable to countermagic. The user considers **two or three structural wound states**, but explicitly defers the number, thresholds, and actual break sequence until structure-creation mechanics are designed. Black reduction to magical barrier Power remains relevant; exact interaction with a partially broken structure remains open.
- **Open for calibration:** Exact directional arc (90° versus 180° was suggested but not approved); facing/turning and slow-infiltration timing checks; whether a shield's Power subtracts before or after other defenses; the detailed multiple-hit shield contest; base spell Power, MP and Channel costs; barrier geometry, transparency, structure wounds, and relationship between Green-created physical matter and White walls; Concordance's specialist superior protection. Do not use any earlier illustration or experimental calculator as final canon.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Spiritus astral projection consequences and Warlock full-system development (LATEST)

**Author confirms the following qualitative/numerical Soul-Shaping / Spiritus projection mechanics** for the planned modular Warlock system, superseding tentative alternative wording:
- **Abandoned physical body:** helpless while astrally projected.
- **Components:** the **Cognum and Anima transfer** into the incorporeal projection. The **Spiritus does not fully transfer**; it **stretches from the body and tears off a piece**, replacing what is lost through a **high upfront mana expenditure**. Preserve the established Corpus as physical body and avoid describing the entire Spiritus as freely detached. Exact Spiritus restoration, fragmentation and interaction with Soul Shred remain unresolved.
- **Short range:** a **visible tether** links projection and body, allowing the projected spirit to use **mana accessible to its host/body**. Warlocks retain **zero natural MP**; they still may shape accessible external mana, so this does not imply the host possesses a new innate MP pool.
- **Tether defense and severing:** the tether has **1 wound of durability**, uses the **projected spirit's Endurance**, and a **non-blunt attack against the tether incurs +1 difficulty**. If the tether is severed/destroyed, the spirit **immediately returns** to the body and the **body takes 1 wound**. Other attack/targeting/defense rules remain ordinary until further adjudication.
- **Long range:** projected spirit can **still shape mana from lawful accessible external sources**, including Warlocks; however it **cannot access the host body's mana through the short-range tether** and suffers an **approximately 50% reduction across all attributes**, pending the exact multiplier/rounding mechanics. No free generation, harvesting bypass or universal remote source access is implied.
- **Destruction of the spirit's projection:** whether short or long range, **the spirit reunites with its physical body and the body receives 3 wounds**, reflecting transferred soul injuries. For long-range projection, reunification takes **approximately one minute**. The user explicitly says three wounds usually incapacitates rather than kills outright, but actual survival still follows applicable wound rules and pre-existing injuries. **Short-range projected-spirit destruction is distinct from cutting its tether**: spirit destruction costs **3 wounds**, tether severance **1 wound**. Exact return timing for short-range spirit destruction is OPEN; do not import long-range one-minute delay automatically.
- **Future calibration:** upfront mana cost, tether reach/visibility, long-range boundary, projection duration, actions, targeting incorporeal beings, Spiritus restoration and Soul Shred interactions, rounding of ~50% attribute reductions, reentry, prior bodily wounds and defenses remain OPEN. No final MP/Channel Limit formula approved.

**Author confirms the preceding foundational Manashifting directions:** broad Red energy enhancement, Blue temporary nonchemical morphological adaptation to actual circumstances, Green physical/living repair and object growth, White order/finite overshield, Black entropy/darkness; Mana-Shaping, Spell-Shaping and eventually Soul-Shaping development, with Craft-unique Esotery; 100%-efficient lawful color-compatible Leywell filling. Soul creation and destruction remains deferred until other Crafts are developed.

**Design priority:** Carry Warlock/Manacraft through **a complete top-to-bottom modular calibration**, including Warlock-specific universal discipline applications, its external mana handling/economy, spell modifiers and full Craft-specific progression. Previous approved Manacraft skill fees/ledger remain authoritative until explicit migration. Proposed MP, SP, Power, object-creation and Channel Limit equations must be labeled exploratory until approved.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: Foundational Manashifting, Warlock disciplines and soul projection (LATEST)

**Status:** Author-approved qualitative direction for the **planned** modular Manacraft migration. Preserve existing approved historical five Warlock discipline/rank/SP/MP ledger until explicitly migrated; do not invent replacement numerical mechanics, no free abilities beyond explicit new conceptual directions.

**Fundamental raw mana expressions (not an exhaustive list):**
- **Red — Energize:** Enhances/transfers available energy through a relevant target or medium: a thrown rock accelerates, a burning object grows hotter, an electrical source can discharge more violently; a living target may be *sped up*, not automatically damaged. Red should interact with actually present energy/material rather than grant arbitrary source-free radiation, electricity, or other exotic energy. Unstructured destructive projection remains possible with lawful mana.
- **Blue — Adapt/Morph:** Temporarily alters **shape and physical arrangement**, not chemical composition. Flexible matter such as rope may move or weave in intricate ways; form changes can affect equipment. Organism adaptations are **responsive to immediate conditions rather than selected freely by caster**: e.g., gills under water, a survival change during freefall/crushing, but no arbitrary wings or muscle enhancement outside a qualifying threat. Blue can redirect energy and produce crude emotion/perception attacks; the previously approved wounded-target sensory-overload/pain and coordination effects remain.
- **Green — Grow/Heal/** **Repair** (replace any use of *regenerate* in the newly proposed five-color expression list with **repair** so objects are explicitly eligible). Green can heal or repair, stimulate growth, and create/expand natural material **including stone**, using mana and the emerging universal object-creation system. Novice/low-investment stone may be porous, impure, air-filled and weak; very high investment and skill can create extraordinarily dense/strong stone. Quality, volume, density, resistance and costs follow the future object-creation framework. Neither biological-only restriction nor automatic unlimited perfect matter creation is intended.
- **White — Organize/Order:** Grants a finite, overwhelmable **all-angle overshield** rather than automatically stacking huge END bonuses; may improve accuracy, clarity and cognition, increase radiant light, and **organize unordered physical items when spread through surrounding air** (e.g., tidy a messy pile). Exact sorting logic, scope and overshield calculations remain open. White restoring a confused person's coherence is a suitable effect, not automatic negation of every hostile mental condition.
- **Black — Degrade/Entropy:** Weakens, decays, exhausts, suppresses light to make darkness and deteriorates structures. Aiming into an existing wound may inflict more harm than an equivalent basic Red blast. Condition buildup and comparisons are still unpriced.

**Manashifting baseline:** Warlock raw mana manipulation remains broadly improvisational. Learned specialties expand its interactions and limitations rather than charging a rank for every trivial aim, move, shape or use. Keep actual external mana supply, eligible targets/materials, resistance and existing handling/resource rules. The above corrects exploratory claims that red necessarily injures creatures or Green is biological-only.

**Proposed Craft-specific specialties (direction, rank progressions OPEN):** **Mana-Shaping** explores unusual targets (air, organs, internal spaces), novel raw-mana expressions and eventual combinations of two mana colors into emergent effects; **Spell-Shaping** counters/rebounds selected colors, actively and deliberately reshapes allied spells by adding one's own investment/shaping, and eventually infuses different mana colors into existing spell structures. It **does not passively share all Warlock skills with the party**; every modification requires active shaping or mana investment and lawful access. **Soul-Shaping** concerns the four established soul components and finally the whole soul, but full mechanical design and high-level soul creation/destruction/restructuring are **DEFERRED pending development of Summoners, Cogmarshals and other peers**; don't finalize the five-rank placement yet. Craft-specific **Esotery** is a **discipline family every Craft may have, but the effects and purchased ranks are unique to that Craft**, not a universal cross-Craft skill; Wakecraft water optics do not give Pyromancy Brimstone.

**Soul-Shaping qualitative directions:** **Anima**'s main support application is to **transfer actual externally held compatible-color mana into another creature's natural MP pool**; recipient's mana color must match. **Do not include the earlier suggestion to change mana affinities**. Other established Anima casting-suppression possibilities may be explored. **Cognum** memory editing, theft and fabrication; **Corpus** bodily sculpting; **Spiritus** astral projection; high-tier integrated soul work may reshape, create or destroy souls, potentially with materials including other souls, but no mass-soul-merging or automatic power creation is approved as a finished technique. Soul integrity, free will, resisting, ethics and Starspawn exceptional protection must receive future treatment.

**Spiritus astral projection:** At short range, a projected incorporeal spirit remains attached to its body by a **visible tether**, enabling access through the tether to **mana held by its host/body**. At long range projection lacks access **through the tether** to the host body's mana and incurs **substantial penalties across attributes** (exact bands/costs/ranges OPEN). This does **not** cancel the Warlock-specific **0 natural MP** rule or create free spellcasting: the precise interaction for Warlocks accessing **external** mana while projected is explicitly OPEN. Resolve which soul components project, body vulnerability, tether severing, ordinary incorporeality, death, range, target contest, permission and reentry before final mechanics.

**Universal Transformation:** An eligible learned form/imbuement may be sustained for hours or potentially permanently, with major cost and **Soul Strain as a possible permanent-effect tradeoff**. The Craft must first possess the adaptation/effect and deliver its lawful medium; universal Transformation does not hand everyone Blue morphology or unrestricted biology. Numerical duration, costs and strain remain OPEN.

**Warlock mana economy:** The author's reaffirmed **existing sources** include willing allies, accessible ongoing spells and Leywells; universal Medium advancement opens increasingly unusual sources. **Warlocks can fill a properly color-matched conventional Leywell at 100% transfer efficiency**, meaning no mana is lost to a transfer tax, **not** that they conjure mana or ignore handling, capacity, permissions, timing or the Leywell's strict single-color identity. Specialized Primordial Leywells remain separate. This is a new explicit efficiency ruling; exact action/throughput and mana-timing mechanics remain for modular integration.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CLARIFICATIONS: Pattern Weaving, specialized Primordial Leywells, and Spirit Anchors (LATEST)

**Pattern Weaving:** A captured spell pattern is held **in one hand together with the mana of the original spell**. For example, on intercepting a blue Psychic spell, a Warlock may hold its original blue mana and pattern for subsequent lawful replication, **or fling it back as raw blue mana** rather than preserve/cast the pattern. A pattern is not a separately transferable store independent of that original mana; placing it in a container loses the pattern. Ordinary one-hand Handling Capacity, temporary-holding limits, contested capture, MP minimums, modifiers and effect scaling remain relevant; exact recast expenditure/timing and how a counterprojection interacts with hand limits remain open.

**Dispel:** The author expressly agrees with the previous interpretation: it seizes/disrupts an incoming spell's medium rather than harvesting its mana, with the approved twice-effective disruptive comparison and four actions or two actions plus one self-inflicted wound. Precise contest, reaction readiness and timing remain for modular spell calibration.

**Primordial Mana:** The author accepts **new, specialized Leywells** intended to hold prepared colorless Primordial Mana. Existing ordinary **single-color** Leywells still only accept their matching original colors. The specialized variant's construction, capacity, costs, input compatibility and safeguards remain to define, rather than granting an automatic conversion to existing ordinary Leywells.

**Ghost Mana, ghosts, and Phantoms:** Manifestation is **not simply location-based**. A lingering ghost or Phantom has a **Spirit Anchor**, an object deeply meaningful to that individual in life. The Spirit Anchor is necessary to its ghostly persistence/manifestation. **Destroying the Spirit Anchor releases the ghost permanently**, creating high stakes for its protection. A ghost that fails to absorb enough mana **goes dormant within its Spirit Anchor** until the anchor is touched, whereupon it **siphons the mana needed to manifest**. Ghosts commonly have **imperfect, emotionally shaped memories**. Hostile ghosts remain **Phantoms**, without automatic loyalty to their summoner, feeding on others' mana and pursuing unfinished business. Exact touch activation, energy demand, resistance, whether object damage short of destruction affects a ghost, dispelling versus true release, and exception handling remain open. Original ordinary Cognum decay remains the default; lingering ghosts are exceptional.

**Modular-spellcasting calibration backlog (not new numerical approvals):**
- *Universal:* action/MP/modifier scaling, Channel Limit and automatic Craft Mastery equation, attack Power and applicable protections, interaction between Power versus Range versus area versus effect count, exact consolidated Magnitude five-rank allocation, duration/upkeep/Autonomy triggers and remote scry ranges, temporary/permanent Transformation resistance and Execute adjudication.
- *Medium:* precise physical-material extraction throughput, source depletion, hot/cold ash, Hearthiron thermal-equivalence accounting, spell eligibility, connection sensing, and high-rank medium supply.
- *Wakecraft Esotery:* Mirrorwake evidence clarity and reach; Surface Dominion physical durability; Refraction illusion checks; Cloudcraft long-term ×2 efficiency and mobility/slow strengths; Abyssal Pressure output and resistance.
- *Pyromancy Esotery:* Breath of Flame oxygen enrichment/depletion as a function of **Power (not Range)**, spell MP modifiers, immediate fire-spread, suffocation, explosion thresholds; Ghostfire material choices; Brimstone creation durability/explosive Power, detonation and ash usage (its volatility is **not adjustable**); Ashen Reconstruction hot/cold quality, material and Phoenix Reversal timing; Cursed Flame material selection, combustion rate, oxygen dependence and sustain fee.
- *Warlock Esotery:* Ghost Mana emotional/word-memory resolution, Spirit Anchor manifestation, drain/siphon rate, dormancy and release; Pattern Weaving hand capacity/timing, minimum recast, original-pattern modifiers, raw-mana return, and transfer destruction; Dispel doubled contest, readiness and actions/wound; Mana Conduit END/MP and material-AR/MR tradeoffs; Primordial Mana five-color input ratios, 2× potency placement, random output distribution, Wild Magic event probabilities, specialized Leywell crafting/holding capacity.
- *Interface with existing Manacraft:* preserve approved Warlock zero innate MP, 30/60-second hand holding, END-based one-hand cap, existing absorption contest, original five discipline/skill ledger, Leywell strict color identities, and Soul Reaping special Execute cost/yield pending formal integrated migration.

**Workflow:** Complete universal modular spellcasting fundamentals before assigning unapproved numerical formulas or converting the historical SP/rank ledgers; approved thematic/rank directions are not a fully migrated price catalogue.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Warlock Esotery ranks I–V (LATEST)

**Status:** Explicitly selected qualitative five-rank Esotery direction. Detailed mana costs, Power/resistance math, duration, SP ledger integration, numerical capacity interactions, and modular spell mechanics remain **OPEN**. This new proposed Esotery progression does not silently delete or rewrite the older approved five Warlock Manacraft skill tracks or their existing fees.

**I — Ghost Mana:** Warlocks may extract from objects and surroundings primarily **emotional imprints** of events. At low Power, a murder weapon might convey ominous emotion or a sense of wrongdoing; at higher Power, distinct fragments of spoken words may emerge. Objects are not intelligent or omniscient witnesses. At sites of especially great tragedy, a Warlock may seek to summon a true lingering **ghost**, where a departed creature's **Cognum has stubbornly adhered to the nearby environment**. Hostile ghosts are **Phantoms**. Summoning creates **no loyalty, obedience, or safety**: Phantoms feed on others' mana and seek to resolve unfinished business. Actual conditions for Cognum persistence, ghost intelligence, manifestation, summoning, resisting, feeding, and dismissal are not quantified. Ordinary Cognum decay at death remains the default; persistent ghosts are exceptional.

**II — Pattern Weaving:** When draining mana from an existing spell, the Warlock may also take its **spell pattern into one hand**. While personally holding the pattern, pushing mana through that hand **recreates the spell with the original caster's modifiers**. The Warlock may invest more or less mana in mana-scalable aspects, including Power, but **must spend at least the minimum mana necessary to cast the spell**; lower expenditure cannot erase the original rank/prerequisite or minimum costs. **Storing the captured pattern in a container destroys it.** Pattern Weaving does not teach the Warlock the spell permanently or grant general conventional spellcasting. Compatibility with the one-hand Handling Capacity, safe 30-second/absolute 60-second ordinary holding duration, source colors, spell eligibility, magical medium/delivery and Channel Limit is unresolved; do not invent a bypass or a new storage method.

**III — Dispel:** The Warlock seizes the **medium** of an incoming spell rather than absorbing its mana. Relative to ordinary absorption the disruptive effect is **twice as powerful**, but the spell's mana is forcibly blasted outward as **dissipated, unusable mana** instead of being kept by the Warlock. **Requires a total of four channeling actions**, or **two actions if the Warlock willingly suffers one wound**. Clarify later how the medium seizure, doubled Power, interruption timing, ordinary absorption contests, action prep, target selection and emitted energy behave; this is not an automatic all-purpose reaction, unresisted cancellation, mana gain, or a replacement for ordinary medium-seizure rules.

**IV — Mana Conduit:** The Warlock changes the **magical reactivity** of matter and living creatures, increasing or decreasing it at the expense of corresponding physical properties. At maximum effect, even **Glassweave** may reverse its normal physical-versus-magical robustness, becoming physically very strong and magically weak. Applied to a creature, one direction sharply **reduces END while multiplying maximum mana reserve by more than compensates for that decrease**; the reverse **strengthens END while shriveling the mana reserve**. Exact attribute tradeoff, minimums, defenses, duration, eligibility and material-specific MR/AR formula are OPEN. Warlocks' approved **zero natural MP** and one-hand Handling Capacity remain unchanged by default: creature mana-capacity modification does not silently give Warlocks internal mana storage.

**V — Primordial Mana:** Combine **all five existing colors of mana** into a single **colorless** mana form. This does **not** create a sixth ordinary mana color or permit using Primordial Mana as any chosen color. When it produces an effect, the **manifested mana color is random**, its output effects are **twice as potent** as ordinary mana, and Wild Magic may cause bizarre additional phenomena, including **small portals to the Celestial World** and unexpectedly intricate spell effects. Because access to all five input colors is difficult in battle, Warlocks commonly **prepare it ahead of time and store it in a Leywell**. Ordinary Leywells' established *one-color-only* acceptance conflicts with assuming they can hold colorless Primordial Mana: the author's pre-storage intention is approved, but **the requisite Leywell exception, special receptacle, or construction specification is OPEN**, so do not claim an ordinary single-color Leywell automatically supports colorless storage. Input amounts, conversion loss, actual random color distribution, wild-effect probabilities, multiplier placement and portal rules remain OPEN.

**Prior Pyromancy clarification (same conversation):** For **Breath of Flame**, increasing spell Power increases the **degree of oxygen concentration or removal within a small space**, **not the spell's Range**. Range and effect area are governed separately by the universal modular spell disciplines; no numeric concentration or suffocation threshold is yet approved.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Fire Esotery Breath of Flame, Ghostfire, Brimstone, Ashen Reconstruction and Cursed Flame

**Author-approved qualitative Fire Esotery design (November 19, 2026):** Rank I **Breath of Flame** manipulates and relocates oxygen, including extraction from water. Oxygen-rich pockets amplify fire Power and permit immediate flame spread; oxygen-poor pockets suppress/extinguish fire and increase MP costs of spells cast within because of physical strain. Stronger oxygen-rich zones can become latent explosive hazards and can slightly *reduce* spell costs through improved physical ability. Removing breathable air causes an immediate suffocation hazard for exposed creatures. Oxygen is a distinct, manipulable **Breath of Flame medium only**, not an additional source of fire/smoke medium and not a means of fueling other Pyromancy spells; the caster spends mana to reposition/control oxygen. Combining Breath of Flame with appropriate **Transformation** can create or remove oxygen within a living body, requiring the normal Transformation contact, resistance, costs and other applicable requirements; external oxygen manipulation alone is not unrestricted internal body manipulation. The precise enrichment/depletion thresholds, affected spell costs, amplification, explosive triggers, volumes, durations, atmospheric behavior, suffocation procedure and defense/resistance remain **OPEN**; no free area execute or unopposed instant death is implied.

Rank II **Ghostfire** permits magical flame to traverse selected **nonliving materials** without setting them alight or damaging them, while still burning creatures (including allies) and other unprotected matter; example: attack creatures on a rope bridge without burning the bridge. Selection scope, contact effects and spell pricing remain open. Rank III **Brimstone** uses real ash as mandatory medium, made solid through heat and mana. All Brimstone is inherently **volatile**, with **no caster control over degree of volatility**. It is exceptionally durable/stable **only on the turn created**; afterward it is explosively unstable, requiring a triggering impact/attack or ignition/heat to detonate rather than automatically exploding. Blast calculations, storage, trigger specifics, environmental interactions and safety are open. Rank IV **Ashen Reconstruction** rebuilds from ash: **still-hot ash** carries an intact memory permitting perfect reconstruction; once cold, its memory degrades and reconstruction is crude. **Phoenix Reversal** is the pinnacle pre-destruction application, cast beforehand on an object or person so that later reconstruction is possible; it is not a blanket free resurrection, and its cost, eligibility, timing, recovery, consent/resistance and relationship to death rules remain open. Rank V **Cursed Flame** targets a material named at casting, allowing that normally nonflammable substance to burn as though rich in fuel. Tougher materials, such as steel, burn longer rather than vanishing instantly; it requires normal oxygen and ongoing spell maintenance. Ordinary defenses and spell contests are not silently bypassed. These are approved *ability directions*, not a finalized numerical SP/casting ledger.

**Supersedes the earlier exploratory Brimstone notion of choosing or regulating volatility:** the caster **cannot** tune how volatile Brimstone becomes. No final numeric mechanics or Skill Point migration approved.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Wakecraft Esotery ranks I–V; Mirrorwake compressed, Cloudcraft introduced

The author chooses **I Mirrorwake** (read water-preserved memories, even those thousands of years old if meaningfully present, plus remote water scrying; preserve practical novice investigation precedent, not old per-rank age/clarity lockouts); **II Surface Dominion** (water becomes moldably solid with **about half the toughness of ice**); **III Refraction** (manipulate light via water for optical distortion, projected imagery and concealment); **IV Cloudcraft** (mold water vapor, fog and clouds as though solid; **twice as mana-efficient when delivering long-term effects through clouds**; combination with Surface Dominion permits standing/walking on cloud or fog constructs, and cloud/fog manipulation can severely impede movement); **V Abyssal Pressure** (extraordinary pressure from water beyond its ordinary volume/depth). The user rejected requiring a novice Mirrorwake reader to obtain only vague impressions from ancient water: old RP already included meaningful memories from a millennia-old bottle, and that experience is to be preserved. Water must actually retain the relevant memories; automatic omniscience, unconstrained reconstruction and decoding Cipherlight are not granted.

**Scope:** This is an approved future Esotery progression, **not yet a complete replacement skill-point ledger**. The older standalone Mirrorwake techniques are superseded as a proposed *five-purchase gateway* to meaningful ancient memories or scrying, but retain historical reference value and unconflicted evidence/Cipherlight limits until full migration. Learned Cloudcraft at IV is an **express exception** to the ordinary direct-liquid-only Wakecraft constraint, not a free basic vapor-control ability. Exact half-ice structural mechanics, upkeep/mana-efficiency accounting, fog movement penalties, Abyssal Pressure damage/defense and ranks' MP/Channel Limit costs remain unapproved.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Hearthiron twelvefold thermal medium and half-range extraction

The author corrects Hearthiron's proposed Banked Heat thermal-medium equivalence from **at least ×3 to ×12 ordinary tile-space equivalence**, specifically for even a small quantity of Hearthiron, provisionally subject to later upward revision. The separate general Banked Heat **at least ×3 per comparable heated tile** direction is unchanged. This does **not** multiply approved Hearthiron equipment charges, damage, or price.

The author also **approves the previous recommendation** that the ordinary Medium extraction/control area extends **half as far as a caster's ordinary attack spell Range**; resource may be harvested when directly perceived or when **continuously connected to perceived eligible medium**, but only **inside** that control radius. Fire and smoke in an eligible connected forest-fire network need not be visually identified tile by tile. Connected material beyond the radius is excluded from ordinary extraction; exceptional learned remote operations may be developed separately. Using proposed Magnitude distances gives 15/45/150/500 feet, half-mile, 5 miles. Actual extraction needs lawful throughput, cost, containment and release; awareness is not unlimited resource handling. Formal spell rank and SP migration remains unapproved.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR MEDIUM CORRECTIONS: Deepdraw rank order, ash extraction, Banked Heat/Hearthiron concentration

The author directs the **planned** Wakecraft Medium ladder to swap prior proposed Rank III Buried Spring with Rank IV Borrowed Moisture; revised order I Dewcatcher, II Pure Current, III Borrowed Moisture, IV Buried Spring, V The Last Drop. **Original canonical Deepdraw rank order remains in the historical reference until the overall skill-ledger migration is approved.** Pyromancy proposal **Kindling is rejected** in favor of **Rank II Ash Extraction**, harvesting **ash substance separately from heat**, from both Hot and Cold Ash. Proposed Rank III **Banked Heat** must contain **at least 3× ordinary fire's thermal-medium per comparable tile**; even a little **Hearthiron** has **at least 3× the normal size-equivalent heat reservoir**. This does not itself alter existing equipment charge counts/attacks. Need heat unit/conversion, ash behavior and eligible spell-medium definitions later.

The author additionally proposes **Medium extraction/control radius equal to half ordinary spell attack range**, with access to medium that is **directly sensed or continuously connected to sensed medium**, including connected flames/smoke across a forest fire, only inside the area of control. This half-range and connectivity mechanic is **under discussion**: no final numeric approval yet. Attacks, active extraction, remote sensing, and spell Power must be kept distinct; limits for extraction throughput, ordinary resource cost and excess medium disposal remain open.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CORRECTION: Hero/Legend Execute divisor at three and four wounds

The author clarifies that **Hero/Legend Executes have a separate wound-gated Power divisor**: automatically resisted at **0–2 wounds**, **÷3 Power at 3 wounds**, **÷2 at 4 wounds**, and **÷1 at 5+ wounds** using the same progression and minimum divisor of one. Ordinary Execute targets retain **÷4, ÷3, ÷2, ÷1 at 0, 1, 2, 3+ wounds** respectively; the minimum ÷1 resolves the otherwise-zero divisor at four or more. Older tentative universal wound scaling and earlier suggestions that Heroes/Legends reach ÷1 at three wounds are **superseded**. The author also confirms the intended Warlock Soul Reaping reward is mana of the victim's **soul color**, not 'soil color'. Specific Execute resistance calculations, MP variants, and Soul Reaping yield are still open.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Executes as universal non-wound finishers; Soul Reaping executes instead of XP damage

The author establishes the name **Execute** for spells with a non-wound-based instant-kill or scene-long incapacitation outcome if the target fails to resist. An ordinary Execute starts at **10 MP** and **one-quarter of an equivalent regular attack spell's Power**. **Each existing target wound lowers the fourfold Power divisor by one**; ÷4, ÷3, ÷2, ÷1 for zero through three wounds is the most direct reading, but **the 4+ wound arithmetic is mathematically undefined and requires explicit approval of a floor**. **Heroes and Legends automatically resist Executes below three wounds**; at three or more wounds they face normal applicable resistance. No universal free contact, unavoidable instant death, or exemption from underlying medium, targeting and channeling prerequisites is approved. The exact resistance procedure, injury-stage accounting, effect duration edge cases and any spell-specific surcharge are open.

**Warlock Soul Reaping revision:** Mana Harvest V Soul Reaping is now a **lethal Execute**, rewarding a **massive amount of mana of the victim soul's color** on success and **not causing XP damage**. The author wrote 'color of the soil', likely meaning 'soul'; confirm terminology rather than treating it as literal soil. Replace prior soul-capture/vessel procedure insofar as inconsistent with lethal execution, but preserve existing exceptional protections for Starspawn Astral Hearts and Celestial Recall pending explicit review. Existing 20-MP activation/5 MP upkeep wording needs reconciliation with new general 10 MP Execute base; total mana yield and delivery mechanics unapproved. **Wakecraft fatal extraction of a target's bodily water** is a named Execute application with its own medium/contact conditions pending detailed rules. Other distinct XP/soul-damage systems (e.g. Celestite) are not changed by this Soul Reaping-specific instruction.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Independent reach for Scry and magical perception

The author approves the earlier recommendation that **Autonomy Rank IV Scry should not automatically inherit ordinary spellcasting Range**, explicitly including the perception/sensory aspect. Craft-specific magical perception also has its own appropriate sensory scope rather than automatically using the Magnitude/Range spell-placement ladder. Exact Scry and perception distances, eligibility, and mechanics remain unapproved and require calibration. This clarification changes no existing approved numerical range limits or spellcasting reach mechanics.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR DECISION: Autonomy revised to Perception and Scry; no INT sacrifice

The author approves **Autonomy affecting independence rather than duration**. Rank I **Persistence** enables effects to continue without active maintenance, but does not inherently extend spell duration. Rank II is **Perception**, replacing the earlier **Instructions** proposal: an autonomous spell gains its caster's applicable environmental and magical perception, but **may only be instructed to activate on perceiving a specified trigger** at this rank. Rank III **Reactivity** builds on that perception by enabling more responsive behavior, rather than merely providing the first trigger. Rank IV **Scry** replaces the proposed Independence rank, letting the caster perceive through and remotely control a spell, potentially issuing new commands. Rank V **Intelligence** enables genuinely intelligent autonomous effects with additional mana expenditure, **not a temporary reduction in the caster's INT**. Magnitude continues to govern simultaneous spell count independently. Open details: rank II senses and limits, trigger complexity, rank III reactive scope, rank IV remote range and retasking limits, rank V mana, durations and upkeep. Older alternative rank names/examples were proposals only and are superseded by this direction.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CORRECTION: Transformation delivery via imbued medium; permanent effects stringent

The author's latest decision **supersedes the earlier interpretation that a living body itself automatically qualifies as the magical medium**. Transformation is an **ordinary spell** whose alteration must be **conveyed through qualifying medium contact with its target**. **Wakecraft:** a soaked target or one hit by a lawful water attack. **Pyromancy:** target already Burning, or contacted by a new qualifying fire attack. **Psycraft:** appropriate mandatory focusing crystal/gem AND successful Cipherlight contact. Eligibility/delivery is **distinct from successful transformation**; it neither waives MP, Channel Limit, action costs nor grants uncontested hostile alteration. Precise duration of delivery eligibility, hit-versus-contact requirements and defenses are open.

The author envisages **gem imprisonment through Cipherlight transformation, temporary gas transformation, and disintegration into ash** as high-level possible applications, not prepriced finalized spells. **Permanent transformations must be among the most expensive and easiest-to-resist workings in the game**. Set concrete MP, capacity, resistance, reversibility and continuity rules later. Earlier Transformation rank framework (Control, Hostility, Adaptation, Alteration/Projection, Transmogrification), including rank III resistance modifications, remains in development; existing historical rank/SP ledgers are not replaced.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: Five-rank Transformation scope and third-rank elemental resistances

The author accepts the emerging five-rank **Transformation** design, with a major clarification: **Transformation may target other creatures, not only the caster**, including allies and enemies, and generally leans toward supportive effects. Qualifying bodies can serve as the relevant **medium for learned Transformation effects**. Pyromancy examples include turning a hostile creature's foot into fire (possible trip, then legitimate fire propagation) or granting allies safe smoke respiration. This does not approve automatic hits, uncontested hostile bodily alteration, unrestricted bodily-medium access for other spells, or free bypass of mana, Channel Limit and defenses.

The progression direction is I **Control**, II **Hostility**, III **Adaptation**, IV **Alteration/Projection** (hybrid), V **Transmogrification**. Rank III **also unlocks separate spell applications that increase or decrease the applicable element's physical resistance or magical resistance**, not a single combined all-resistance modifier; exact distinctions by Craft and numeric tuning remain open. Prior examples retained: Pyro temperature regulation/burning body, Wake internal water regulation and blood-use/clotting, Psychic self-insight/body-delivered psychic influence, higher adaptation and body reshaping, and ultimate partial or full elemental embodiment. No new SP price, Channel Limit threshold, duration, target resistance procedure or defense coefficient is approved. Preserve historical Craft ledgers pending explicit migration.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR DECISION: Combine Range and Magnitude, retain accepted numerical benchmarks

The author explicitly directs **merging Range with Magnitude into a single universal magical discipline**, rather than spending separate Skill Points on two mostly prerequisite-like progressions. The accepted **design benchmarks** are base casting range **30 ft**, extension steps **90 ft, 300 ft, 1,000 ft, 1 mile, 10 miles**, and area radii **30 ft (early), 300 ft (middle; 10× the former range), 2 miles (high)**, plus **2 and 8 simultaneous sustained effects** at two multiplicity milestones, scaling with automatic Craft Mastery. If remote placement range is insufficient, a very large lawful area may be **caster-centered at casting time**. These figures are accepted as targets; **their exact merged five-rank allocation is still a proposal**. No MP/Channel Limit costs, upkeep, Mastery scaling, or spell-shape geometries were finalized. Preserve the existing approved Wakecraft Longwake ranks and Craft SP ledger until an explicit migration is approved. Universal magic currently has **five proposed disciplines**: merged Magnitude, Medium, Autonomy, Esotery, Transformation; free perception develops with Channel Limit as a design direction rather than a purchased discipline. Do not silently canonize unfinished progression.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR DECISION: Automatic magical Craft Mastery replaces separate Mastery purchase proposal

The author explicitly selects **automatic mastery**: advancing a magical Craft's learned disciplines increases that Craft's Mastery and therefore its **Channel Limit**, without a separate Mastery skill purchase. A **discipline rank is a strict prerequisite** for its spells and modifiers, while **Channel Limit is a separate capacity requirement** for each actual working. A partial transformation such as turning a hand into water to extend reach can require less capacity than full-body transformation; no numeric capacity requirements are approved. The author has also directed **halving the INT-derived Channel Limit component** and adding craft-specific learned Mastery, but the exact arithmetic, rounding, contribution per rank, multicraft accounting, and compatibility with legacy formulas/special Craft exceptions are **unresolved**. Preserve existing one-action and two-action allocation and other resource gates pending formal numerical adoption.

**Progression context, not yet finalized as a replacing skill ledger:** Target six universal magical disciplines and three craft-specific disciplines, each considered for five ranks. Universal magical principles do not apply automatically to martial or crafting Crafts such as Jinx. Ordinary SP remains shared with prospective proficiencies; 'High Power Progression' aims for about 2× SP for solo-friendly multicraft breadth, but its implementation and SP schedules are not approved. Do not silently replace existing Craft ranks, SP prices, or prerequisites.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Ash terrain and heat extraction

The author approves **one ash tile left by each extinguished fire tile**. **All ash** costs double normal movement to traverse as difficult terrain. Ash younger than **one minute** is **Hot Ash**, applying **half as much Burning status buildup as fire** for comparable exposure; ash of age one minute or older is **Cold Ash**. A Pyromancer may **absorb heat from one ash square as though it were one smoke square**, making the ash Cold Ash. A Pyromancy **ash-reheating spell** is authorized as a future capability, but no name, MP cost, rank, timing, range, heat quantity, or precise spell mechanics have been approved.

**Still open:** interaction of newly reheated ash with the one-minute timer and ongoing exposure; ash removal or dispersal; precise heat absorption procedure with existing smoke medium rules; overlap with existing Burning timing. These are not grounds to invent new numerical rules. Owners: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, and this changelog.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CLARIFICATION: Cleanse may preempt status effects (LATEST)

**Explicit author decision:** A Wakecrafter can use Cleanse on **any of their own status buildup above zero**, even **before the status has any mechanical effect**. Do not require the buildup to reach a Pressure Point, injury, or other activation threshold first. This is permission to cleanse trace/pre-effect buildup, **not** a new reaction, interrupt, immunity, or out-of-turn action: the existing **1 MP, one available action, ordinary-attack-equivalent reduction**, and casting constraints remain. **Still unresolved:** whether Cleanse lowers or ends a condition that has *already* imposed mechanical effects; one versus multiple status pools per use; and the attack-equivalent calculation details. Previous unresolved wording concerning pre-effect eligibility is superseded, while the active-condition question remains open.

---

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Wakecraft Cleanse self-status utility (LATEST)

**Cleanse** costs **1 MP**, uses **one action**, and can only be used when the Wakecrafter is capable of taking that action. It reduces **the caster's own buildup of any status type**, with the reduction **equal to the buildup they would have inflicted by making an ordinary attack with the same action**. It is a non-damaging utility; no automatic additional action, external targeting, or condition immunity follows. This new approved rule is additive to the approved Water Attack, Ice Stab, Freeze Attack, and Shatter.

**Unresolved rather than assumed:** whether a single activation cleanses one selected status buildup or several together, which attack-equivalent offensive buildup calculation applies, and whether removing buildup can cancel a condition that has already triggered. Files updated: `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, and this changelog.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CORRECTION: Wakecraft three attack types and incoming-Power Shatter

**Approved by the author's direct instruction and follow-up clarification:** Water Attack = physical damage + Water Erosion buildup; Ice Stab = physical damage + Freeze buildup; Freeze Attack = **magical energy, zero direct damage of any type**, 2× Freeze elemental status magnitude plus instant conversion of the target's existing Water elemental buildup to Freeze. Water can first erode MR at the separately approved Water thresholds, then be converted into Freeze buildup.

**Shatter correction:** On a Frozen target, a physical hit must qualify to injure **before** applying Shatter; then remove Freeze and **double incoming physical Attack Power before defenses/damage resolution**, **not final damage**. Insufficient hits do not trigger. The earlier provisional interpretation of doubling final post-defense damage is expressly superseded. Status threshold formulas, timing of erosion persistence after conversion, Freeze durations, and detailed magical-defense handling for harmless Freeze status delivery remain open. Subject references: `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`; index updated.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVES Combat Speed for initiative (LATEST)

**Explicit author decision:** Replace **current effective SPD** with **current ordinary Combat Speed** as the initiative ordering statistic. Rank **highest Combat Speed first**. Compute Combat Speed from **effective SPD + effective DEX/4**, with approved worn-armor modifier on the combined value and legitimate wound/equipment-shortfall adjustments. Preserve the approved ties: Heroes/Legends before ordinary combatants; player characters before tied NPC Heroes/Legends; remaining exact ties decided by the DM, with the relative order then fixed. **Ordinary movement remains SPD-based.** The separately approved temporary Dash Combat Speed bonus does not retroactively reorder an existing initiative sequence. Timing for initiative refresh after actual mid-combat changes remains open. **This supersedes older initiative-on-effective-SPD claims** in the November 8 initiative decisions and November 13 Combat Speed scope passages; all other details of those historical approvals remain in force. Update simulator initiative sorting accordingly; no authorization to canonize experimental simulation policies.

**References:** `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.

## November 19, 2026 — AUTHOR CONFIRMS Water-element MR erosion at 1× and 4× SR (LATEST)

**Explicit user clarification:** Water's erosion amounts are decided. At **≥1× target's applicable Water-typed Status Resistance**, **−15% applicable Magic Resistance**; at **≥4×**, **−30% total applicable Magic Resistance** (not −45%, and not a second added −30%). This corrects the preceding references that marked Water erosion percentages as undecided. Water reduces MR rather than AR, and retains the existing Water-specific absence of the ordinary 0.5× Pressure Point. The established legal-contact, three-turn elemental magnitude accumulation/refresh conditions survive unchanged. **Not thereby approved:** any particular MR percentage rounding, instant recovery/persistence, final typed-SR formula including MR/elemental resistance, iterative SR recomputation after erosion, or independent extra physical injury and magic penetration. The numerical Water threshold amounts are canon; previous balance-coefficient or MP-cost proposals remain experimental.

**Files updated on main:** `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, and `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, plus this changelog.

## November 18, 2026 — Pass 2 weapon enchantments and Guardian AUTHOR APPROVED

**Approval:** The author approved the complete 37-entry Pass 2 shortlist: 36 generic weapon enchantments plus Guardian. [Weapon Enchantments](36_Weapon_Enchantments.md) records all approved entries and the fixed slot and rank-by-rank Mixed and Full values. The five fixed three-slot entries are Holdout Weapon, Close Quarters, Concussive, Boomerang and Second Face. Rank Measure occupies one slot.

- **Concussive:** After an eligible connected Blunt Full Attack, spend one injury stage to remove one action on the target’s next turn or two to Stun for the whole next turn. Either trade leaves at least one stage. The approved version replaces the earlier one-stage Stun version.
- **Achilles’ Heel:** Any eligible weapon may use it. Its stacks reduce final Defense Power for the eligible follow-up. END and AR retain their own values.
- **Mantis Blade and Split Rhythm:** The shortlist sets their six slot values and Double Strike triggers. This resolves older references to their calibration as pending.
- **Fire and alternatives:** Existing elemental numbers retain their earlier approval. Sharpness remains the comparison benchmark. Pass 1 entries outside the shortlist and balance-test assumptions retain their prior draft status.

**Still open:** Per-enchantment colors and Leywell MP bands, the final six-slot resource economy, Spellseal’s MR interaction, Guardian’s cross-effect rating and duration matching, Boomerang’s Speed cutoff and delayed-action timing, and underlying trip and disarm procedures.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Medium-equivalent implement ratings, tuning percentages, Soul Strain and dual-focus resource rule (LATEST)

**RATIFIED implement raw equipment ratings by Quality I–V:** **26/30/35/40/48**, each adding independent squared item Power **676/900/1225/1600/2304** to caster attribute Power in the under-development additive spell model. The author explicitly said “I approve” to the proposed Medium-weapon-equivalent ladder, and then “Approved” to the revised tuning/Soul Strain design. This is NOT a retroactive ratification of every candidate physical weapon's experimental additive WR formula or a finished spell MP formula.

**Seven tuneable properties:** Offense, Defense, Utility, Mana Efficiency, Reservoir, Reach, Soul Strain; **Control eliminated**. Each step in offense/defense/utility changes its **implement equipment contribution by 25 percentage points** (not base character attribute Power). Each Mana Efficiency step reduces/increases **actual MP expenditure by 20 percentage points** (positive/negative), not the nominal capacity used against Channel Limit. Reach stays on the list but its exact range and magnitudes are unsettled, so no exploitative free points may be awarded from no-op reductions. The already-approved item point economy remains: 0 initial points, at most two genuine − steps per property, each giving one point; +1/+2/+3 costs 1/3/9 **total**, respectively.

**Finite reservoir and dual-use:** Every legally compatible implement's normal reservoir is Medium Level matching **Quality I–V**, with hard zero to V bounds and need for replenishment. Two legitimately simultaneous implements **may pool the actual quantity of their mutually compatible stored medium and combine their Soul Strain burdens and extra usable mana-capacity effects**, but **one spell uses one implement for its Power/rating/specialization/MP efficiency/Reach**. No extra offensive attack, hand, free recharge or new usable spell is created by dual implements.

**Soul Strain tuning:** Neutral implement has no inherent Soul Strain. Benchmarked to normal enchanted item Quality I–V reference **S=1/2/4/6/8**. −1 adds S actual Soul Strain; −2 adds **2S total**, in exchange for earned tuning points. Positive +1/+2/+3 instead adds **S/2S/3S usable MP reserve capacity**, respectively, adopting the previously floated benchmark-multiple test option by subsequent author approval; does not increase Channel Limit, innate END or Leywell MP. Race-specific interactions and MP rounding remain OPEN.

**Awaiting separately approved design:** exact damage scaling and mana curve, reach modifier, true physical weapon additive law, Psychic focus reservoir substitute, specialized Craft/MP spell Power and defenses. This changelog supersedes the previous entries that indicated independent implement ratings and step magnitudes had not been approved. Files: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, and this changelog.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR CORRECTION: Smoke one-tile limit and additive implement Power (LATEST)

**Smoke:** Author explicitly discards the newly proposed nine-step smoke-count curve. **One 5-foot tile of smoke allows sight but imposes one harder accuracy category**; **more than one tile across the line of sight blocks visibility**. No further tripling penalties or nine-step opacity gate survive. Existing Pyromancer respiration, smoke supply, and legitimate unseen-target guessing/50% attempt are preserved pending precise post-guess targeting interactions.

**Implements:** Author rejects an implement `F` multiplying caster attributes. Casting implement contribution **adds its independent equipment Power to the user's contribution**, benchmarked against **Medium-weapon equipment power across Quality I–V**. The additive weapon model is still marked experimental in existing GitHub references; its separate formal adoption is not presumed from the implement correction. Implement-specific `A² + IR²`, proposed numerical ratings, and specialization increments remain **NOT APPROVED** pending comparison and user authorization. The approved customizable 0-point allocation framework, five physical Quality tiers, finite medium reservoirs and legal dual-implement equipment investment remain unchanged. Existing MP Channel Limit, Crafts, spell prerequisites, enchanted mana supply and combat turn restrictions remain in force. Subject owners updated: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, plus index.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: Nine-step smoke opacity and implement crafting tradeoffs (LATEST)

**Smoke visibility and physiology:** Smoke tile count applies **one harder Naked-d20 accuracy category at 1 tile**, and another each tripling: thresholds **1/3/9/27/81/243/729/2187/6561** for nine steps; more than nine steps blocks sight. An attacker may guess a tile; if correct, a single-target attack has **50% chance to resolve normally**. Clarify line-of-sight smoke counting, how ordinary six-category Impossible interacts with the nine steps, and what 'normally' means for attack accuracy when the target is unseen. Most smoke causes non-Pyromancers trouble only after **minutes of exposure**; full END/environmental effects are still open. **Pyromancers can breathe pure, oxygenless smoke without difficulty**. Existing real fire and fire↔smoke casting rules survive.

**Implements:** The author wants **seven tunable properties**, rather than Light/Medium/Heavy casting-implement divisions; choose a preset or build the item with a **zero-point budget**. Each eligible downgrade (max two per property) grants **1 point**, each upgrade to **+1/+2/+3** costs **1/3/9 total** points. Decrements must matter; no awards after a property reaches its floor. **Every implement** is intended to have an ordinary **finite medium reservoir**, baseline **Medium Level I–V at Quality I–V**, respectively; it cannot exceed V under the stated default cap or be reduced below 0. Decreasing the reservoir reduces Medium Level by one per legal notch, never generating points by reducing level 0. Once spent it must be replenished; no free medium generation from MP. Typical implement requires at least one hand; carrying two is a legal equipment/wealth tradeoff, not an attack/action multiplier. The proper Psychic/Cipherlight reservoir interpretation is open given mandatory gems and no Psychic Refiner.

**Not numerically approved:** Proposed seven-attribute labels, individual power increments/decrements, implement F base by Quality, costs/prices, sample presets, and spell damage formula are all **unapproved designs** to test. Existing mana/Channel Limit, enchantment budgets, conditions, spell and Craft costs, and equipment rules remain untouched. Updated owners: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md`, and index.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Corrected threefold media, basic Pyromantic smoke, Typhoon and opposed seizure (LATEST)

**Medium volumes:** Replaces older **IV–VII = 4/12/36/108** tiles with **IV–VII = 3/9/27/81** tiles. Levels I–III remain **cup/waterskin/one water tile** for Wakecraft and **match/lantern/one fire tile** for Pyromancy. Water quantity **one full tile = three puddles**, superseding four-puddle geometry; a shallow water-tile amount spreads across **three puddle squares**, not four. Casting with a committed source larger than the spell needs costs **one-third of the oversized source**, with recovering remnants subject to physical availability. Dispersed medium still requires **two levels higher**: **III→V 9 tiles, IV→VI 27, V→VII 81**. Existing specific **4 cloud tiles → 1 condensed liquid water tile** is unchanged without separate correction.

**Fundamental Pyromancy:** Fire-to-smoke and smoke-to-fire manipulation is as natural to Pyromancy as changing tone, **not reserved for a smoke-specific learned technique**. Previous references to mandatory specialized smoke ammunition/handling are superseded. Smokescreen extent uses the amount of fire transformed; specific active conversion, fuel/viability and MP timing open. Existing environmental **one smoke tile generated per fire tile each round** survives.

**Typhoon:** All creatures within intense rain have **1 PP and one harder accuracy category**. Wakecrafters use **one puddle-equivalent per rain-filled Typhoon tile**, thus **three tiles = one full concentrated water tile** and continuous renewal while the intense rainfall lasts. This is a stronger rain exception to ordinary dispersed-medium accounting. Exact duration, pressure stacking and phase timing remain open.

**Contested spell control:** Replaces previous deterministic equal/2× seizure guarantees with **opposed effective spell Power informing a Naked-d20 seizure check**. The defending/seizing side has **65% success (Average 8+) at equal Power**. **2×/4×/12× Power advantage = 1/2/3 categories easier**, and opposing superiority makes the check equivalently harder; clamp to approved categories. Current clamp gives **85% at 2×, 100% at 4× and 12×**, or 45%/20%/0% when the seizer is disadvantaged by 2×/4×/12×. **Defensive casting implement bonus** contributes to defensive Power; exact Power formula and integration with Craft skill remain open. **Multiple defenders' Power stacks** for seizure; **multiple attacking casters' Power stacks** for a joint outgoing spell, subject to legal actions, MP and Channel Limits. Existing distinction between medium seizure and complete spell seizure and complete-seizure preparation/action costs must be reconciled in final implementation; no extra free spell is granted.

**Fire hazard:** Fractional 25% round-start fire-spread increments are carried forward rather than rounded up every round. Adjacent target ignition occurs on **d4=1** (25%); mundane hazard is meant to be basic and generally not scale with caster level. Damage, duration, status integration, target exposure timing and ventilation remain uncalibrated.

**Unchanged:** Non-Pyromancy Crafts' preexisting prerequisites, core spell catalogues and purchases, MP, Channel Limit, actual media, finite Refiner storage, physical AR/MR, elemental conditions and two-action combat constraints. Owning documents updated: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and index.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: Seven Medium Levels, dispersed casting and evolving battlefields (LATEST)

**Author establishes a seven-step physical-medium requirement alongside each spell's MP cost.** Wakecraft levels **I–VII: 1 cup water / 1 waterskin / 1 tile / 4 / 12 / 36 / 108 tiles**. Pyromancy **I–VII: a match-sized flame / lantern flame / 1 tile / 4 / 12 / 36 / 108 tiles**. Required medium is expended/committed when casting, though existing water or fire may remain, move, disperse or be re-gathered. Media must be accessible within lawful casting range and not already tied up in a different caster's spell absent seizure. Physical volume of a water tile, portable sizes, and MP-to-Medium Level spell catalogue remain open.

**Dispersed media:** Suitably trained casters can exploit smoke, rain, widespread shallow flooding and clouds with **two higher Medium Levels' worth of dispersed tiles** than the listed concentrated level (III→V 12, IV→VI 36, V→VII 108). A genuine tile of fire creates **one smoke tile each round**; rain tiles replenish each turn while raining; accumulating smoke can support prolonged smoke-technique casting. Subtile and beyond-VII dispersed conversions, smoke timing/retention/ventilation and area counting remain open. Smoke is still a separately learned Pyromancy technique and not a universal substitute for qualifying flame.

**Gathering and environment:** Wakecraft cantrip-level medium acquisition is intended through melting existing ice and condensing **4 cloud tiles into 1 concentrated liquid-water tile**. **1 tile of liquid water spreads into a puddle over 4 squares**; extra water can enlarge puddles and ultimately flood an area, without multiplying total water. Existing liquid-only Wakecraft and no-weather-command restrictions survive. At the **start of each round**, viable fire spreads into flammable material by **25% of existing fire tiles**, along outdoor wind direction or indoor DM-selected random direction. Creatures adjacent to the newly spreading fire roll **d4** against ignition and basic generally non-scaling fire harm. D4 ignition threshold, fractional spreading, damage amount, precise conditions and fuel/ventilation are not yet established.

**Seizure and spell control:** The author describes an **opposed medium-seizure check** using a **defensive casting-implement bonus**. This is not yet a complete numeric replacement for the approved **spell Power thresholds** (>= original for medium control, >= double for complete spell control); reconcile explicitly when contested casting gets a defined roll system. Do not silently grant a free new seizure attempt or waive the existing preparation/action costs.

**Equipment interface:** Finite Refiner Leywells must carry real medium against the seven-step ladder, not percentage discount it. Capacity and replenishment remain uncalibrated. Naga/Psychic gem/Cipherlight mechanics do not automatically adopt the Wakecraft/Pyromancy media ladder. Other spells' costs, SP/ranks, Channel Limits, Shimmer/Elemental Magnitude and combat action economy remain unchanged. Owning files updated: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, and index.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Finite Refiner medium reservoirs (LATEST)

**Author explicitly selects the finite-reservoir model.** Refiner casting implements use a **specialized Leywell** to hold a **large, finite reserve of the Craft's genuine physical medium**. Blue-attuned Refiner stores **liquid water** for Wakecraft; Red-attuned Refiner stores a **viable existing flame** for Pyromancy, with all proper medium requirements preserved. Unlike the discarded percentage-based medium efficiency suggestion, this provides real medium supply and **does not reduce a spell's medium volume or tiles requirement**. Medium can be exhausted or lost, and must be **replenished**, with no automatic limitless regeneration. Keep the Leywell's mana and physical-medium reservoir conceptually distinct; do not infer that all Leywells store matter or create matter from MP.

**Psychic specialization:** Psychics receive **no Refiner**; a different specialization could be proposed later, but a Prism or fixed range bonus is not yet canon. **Open details:** capacity by quality/slots, precise refill procedures and costs, flame viability/fuel, other color-to-medium mappings, implement-specific equipment values and numerical amplifier/conduit/harmonizer comparisons. Earlier spellcasting/channel-action approvals and all real-medium restrictions remain unchanged.

**Subject records updated:** `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`; index updated for navigation.

## November 18, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: Spellcasting implements, distinct Craft scaling and two-action channel budget (LATEST)

**New official action-based Channel Limit:** A spell using **one combat action** may channel no more than **floor(the caster's normal applicable full Channel Limit / 2)**; a spell requiring **both actions** may channel up to **full Channel Limit**. This is a refinement of, not a repeal of, the shared per-turn throughput and prepared-defense reservation system. Respect the two-action turn and **one offensive attack action maximum**, as well as separately established Channel Limit exceptions. Full numerical Channel Limit formulas remain unchanged.

**Magic damage and progression direction:** A character's attack development uses **base attribute, weapon or Craft-specific casting implement, enchantment and combat Craft**, with no fifth automatic damage multiplier. Appropriate casting implements boost their own Crafts and are normally **optional**; **Psychic/Psycraft focusing gems are mandatory**, an explicit vulnerability. Lanterns for Pyromancy and gems for Psychics are example forms. Both **offensive and defensive cantrips** are universally intended weak options at all levels, weaker than mana-spending spells and (for offensive cantrips) comparable weapons. Basic mana-costing spell attacks remain relevant against equal-level opponents even at level 100; greater spells add output and other advantages. **More mana can mean more damage**, but higher output must have meaningful tactical/physical considerations rather than automatically optimal resource efficiency, e.g. bigger or consumed media, danger from enlarged area, or multi-turn charging.

**Approved Craft differentiators:** **Psychic** magic can concentrate high output on a single enemy; **Pyromancy** tends to widen its affected area when pursuing high damage, even when undesirable; **Wakecraft** falls between. Individual area/MP/medium equations, charging requirements and item bonuses have not been assigned. Energy attacks **do not gain a blanket extra raw damage multiplier over otherwise comparable magically produced physical attacks**, because they already confer legitimate **elemental conditions/Elemental Magnitude** and other energy-specific advantages. This **supersedes** the older qualitative directive that energy must also deal more raw damage; physical attacks remain meaningful against high MR, using physical AR where appropriate.

**Preserved and OPEN:** Existing spells and Incantations, spell SP prices, attribute-based Channel Limit calculations, MP reserve rules, real-medium delivery, Psycraft Cipherlight focus, Elemental Magnitude, MR/AR defense, enchantment Leywells, prior specialist exceptions, and combat action economy survive. No numerical cantrip damage, spell MP/Power ladder, implement bonus/slot/Quality scaling, exact medium consumption or blast growth, charge duration, or multi-cast odd-Channel-Limit accounting is approved. Updated owning files: `35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md`, `03_Sorcery.md`, `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and index.
