## November 11, 2026 — Bow construction and Quality V economics accepted as working canon; repricing required

**Author explicitly approves the previously proposed construction identities, physical Quality applications, provisional Quality V prices and comparative direction as canon for now**, adding a mandatory future **price reassessment when enchantments and typical complete character equipment are understood**. The four approved cap, controlled-draw, DEX, full-draw Piercing Power and Close/Effective/Far/Extreme range profiles from prior November 11 rulings are unchanged.

**Construction identities:** High-draw reinforced conventional laminates/wood with Boughsteel fittings; Titan Thornspring elastic limbs and Boughsteel spine; Colossal coupled Thornspring limbs with optional lightweight Trufula construction retaining flammability; Greatbow high-efficiency coupled limbs plausibly using Thornspring, selective Adamantite stress points and lightweight structural pieces. These are current working engineering approaches, not compulsory exclusive recipes, automatic material Power multipliers, invented Smithcraft rank locks or magical effects. Thornspring's existing elasticity is respected; ordinary arrows have no size/force tier.

**Quality refinement, now approved:** Apply existing QI–V requirement factors **1/.95/.90/.85/.80** to *minimum controlled bow draw STR* and *Handling DEX* separately and once, with upward rounding. QV High-draw **28/24**, Titan **64/28**, Colossal **100/32**, Greatbow **144/36**. Quality never alters cap, F/shot Power, range, ammunition counts, intrinsic Pierce ratio, or ordinary shot timing; e.g. QV Greatbow at STR144 yields only **19,440 Piercing Power**. The full QI–V minimum table is in the Ranged Catalogue.

**Working Quality V finished-bow Gem price targets, now approved provisionally:** High-draw **150**, Titan **450**, Colossal **950**, Greatbow **1,750**. These represent comparable typical completed Quality V weapons, not prices for arbitrary enchantment configurations or fixed vendor stock. They do not override the base Quality V **90-Gem** equipment benchmark or level-100 **4,000-Gem** typical equipment portfolio. Graduated specialist/commission availability accepted as direction, without new level gates or universal crafting rarity rolls.

**Author's explicit rebalance note:** Before final pricing, revisit all four price targets after **enchantment pricing and representative equipment loadouts** are designed; account for armor, other weapons, enhancements, consumables and overall wealth allocations. Until then, prices are working canon, not final eternal prices.

**Already settled Piercing arrow mechanics (not newly amended):** Normal Pierce-point **F0.90, 50% covered AR bypass** vs Slashing broadhead **F1.10, 0% bypass**, relative Piercing Power **81/121**, one ordinary arrow per bowshot, no different arrow sizes or Strength tiers. The still-unset arrowhead production, price, quiver capacity and material choices do not authorize another Piercing damage tier or enhanced piercing without further explicit approval.

## November 11, 2026 — Latest author correction abolishes traditional-arrow tiers; bow Power ladder accepted

**Approved correction, highest precedence:** **Do NOT create multiple traditional-arrow sizes, numerical launch-force ratings, STR100/200/300/400 arrow tiers or inventory matching requirements.** This explicitly **supersedes the prior approved working rated-arrow safe-draw scheme**, the `min(STR, bow cap, arrow rating)` law, underdraw-to-lower-rated-arrow procedure and related test scenarios. Historical 784-case ammo comparisons remain archived as superseded calibration, not the current rule. Ordinary arrows work with the currently developed traditional bow ladder without mechanically separate size ratings. Preserve one arrow consumed per normal bowshot; preserve the existing ordinary **Slashing broadhead vs Piercing point** damage-form choice, F1.10 vs F0.90, 0% vs 50% bypass, and 81/121 Piercing-to-Slashing Power relationship. That is a **head/attack-type** distinction, not a size/force rating.

**User approves the other prior recommendations as working bow benchmarks:** advanced high-draw / Titan / Colossal composite / Legendary greatbow individually have cap **100/200/300/400**, minimum controlled effective draw STR **35/80/125/180**, Handling DEX **30/35/40/45** and **Piercing full-draw Powers 14,500 / 45,000 / 90,000 / 150,000**. The high-draw 14,500 was previously approved; **the latter three proposed balanced Powers are now expressly accepted as working calibration** under user's 'otherwise approve all suggestions'. Proper shot draws satisfy `D_actual <= min(effective STR, bow cap)` and `D_actual >= bow's minimum controllable draw`, then use the approved square law based on that bow's full-draw Power. No general upgrade cap or universal ranged Quality Power multiplier. User's approved STR400 engineered-bow goal is achieved.

**Action economy:** Ordinary nock/draw, one arrow per shot, no automatic extra reload turn or free attack, Aim, optic, recoil track or new required setup. Ordinary geometry/sight limits and equipment requirements remain. Previous advanced crossbow, arbalest, firearms, and conventional Slashing/Piercing ammo decisions are otherwise intact; advanced special magic ammunition is still postponed.

**Unresolved:** Final weapon names/material recipes/Quality equipment effects, item-specific distances, economic availability, carrying loads, special nontraditional projectiles. The legendary-high-END armor-relevance observation remains a future playtest ticket, not active armor recalibration.

## November 11, 2026 — Advanced crossbow/firearm handling approved; Slashing/Piercing ammo calibrated; spell ammo deferred

**AUTHOR EXPLICITLY APPROVES previous three-part roadmap and adds a firm special-ammo boundary.** (1) Advanced crossbow is **Support STR28 / Handling DEX30**, hand-cock STR90 or physically suitable lever STR40, one bolt loaded and one dedicated reload turn. Advanced arbalest preserves previously approved **Support STR70 / DEX40 / hand-cock STR170 or winch STR70 / one setup turn / two reload turns**, now explicitly with **no setup reset after normal firing/reloading**, but one fresh setup after physically abandoning or losing its stable deployment. (2) Advanced **precision rifle** is Support STR40 / Handling DEX50 / single-shot Recoil STR70 with **standard magazine capacity5**, and **light precision firearm** is Support STR22 / Handling DEX40 / single-shot Recoil STR38 with **standard magazine capacity6**. Both use one cartridge per normal shot, and one full nonshooting magazine reload turn. Magazine fire does NOT imply automatic gun modes, extra attacks or damage increases. Distinct firearm recoil STR vs Support/DEX and existing accumulated Strain apply unchanged. All are **working item benchmarks** rather than replacements for old entry-shape rows.

**(3) Author specifies only ordinary Slashing vs dedicated Piercing ammunition now.** Adopt the already approved **primary melee damage-form comparison** for matched arrows and physically credible paired crossbow bolts: **Slashing relative F1.10 / 0% bypass**, **Piercing relative F0.90 / 50% covered-AR bypass**. Since Power squares the form factor, Piercing shot Power is **(0.90/1.10)² = 81/121 ≈66.94%** of comparable Slashing Power at identical safe actual draw/mechanical source; not 81.8% Power nor an extra direct 0.90 Power multiplier. Preserve existing published bow14,500, advanced crossbow17,000 and advanced arbalest26,000 **Piercing** test shots; equivalent Slashing algebraic comparisons are about **21,660/25,395/38,840**, not separately authored standalone ammo ratings. Standard rifle15,000 and light firearm11,800 prior shot tests are already genuine Piercing; do not invent ordinary Slashing bullets or an additive 50% armor bypass for nominally 'new' piercing cartridges. No increase to bow cap, arrow form magic, cover penetration, ranges or reload speed. **The prior ammunition-draw-rating scheme is later superseded and removed by explicit author correction.**

**Test summary:** Three previously approved Piercing shot anchors × five defender END values [50,75,100,125,150] × five protection conditions [unarmored, QV medium/heavy Boughsteel, QV medium/heavy Adamantite] = **75** matched-ammunition scenario pairs. Piercing wins impact ratio in **42**, Slashing in **33**, with many tied integer injury results; highlights: END100 high-draw arrow versus unarmored yields Slashing 2 stages, Piercing 1; END100 QV Heavy Adamantite yields Slashing 0, Piercing 1. Tested crossbow shot/reload and precision firearm magazine cycles, and verified normal-fire Recoil Strain uses the existing table. All new numeric item choices are accepted at **working** calibration status; noncanon test result counts are not a new universal encounter distribution.

**Explicit deferral:** Explosive, freezing, spell-infused, elemental and unusual status ammunition wait until relevant spells and magic combat are developed for a fair comparison. Do not invent their costs, blast effects, statuses or multipliers. Final missile geometry (without a traditional-arrow size/rating inventory tier), arrowhead/bolt fit, Gem costs, availability, extra attachment variants, upgraded magazines and ammunition materials remain unapproved. **The legendary bow Powers45k/90k/150k are NOW approved as working calibration under the latest author decision above.** The earlier deferral of ultra-late armor/END work persists. Earlier notes calling *all four* non-arbalest profiles and all ammo form comparisons unresolved are superseded **only for these expressly selected benchmarks**.

**Files changed:** `27_Ranged_Weapon_Catalogue_DRAFT.md` owning detailed profile, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md` combat timing and form ratios, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` equipment cross-reference, `10_Canon_Changelog.md` history and index.

## November 11, 2026 — Advanced sight stability, installation philosophy, and target-only magical glint approved

**Explicit author approval:** Adopt the immediately preceding Quill proposals for **stable/controlled-repositioning/major-disruption** handling, physically compatible and calibrated advanced sights, specialist acquisition and repair expense without invented Gem prices, realistic direct-impact vulnerability, and **no universal added Support STR, recoil penalty or extra setup action** for ordinary advanced sights. Minor controlled aiming and stable support preserve optic advantage; breaking the brace loses its extra Far/Extreme relief while a paid normal Aim can persist if real tracking survives; major disruption breaking aim/tracking cancels both. Stationary actually stable mounts can qualify; ordinary moving saddles do not automatically count. Exact exotic stabilizer models remain open.

**Author addition, approved:** Advanced optics emit a **magical glint perceivable only by the particular intended target**, allowing a target able to perceive it to identify the sniper's firing position. It is **not visible to nearby allies or other observers** as an area glow. On a failed one-shot kill/incapacitation, a surviving capable target can **call out the position**, giving the rest of their group a normal opportunity to respond with cover, counterfire, movement, and Pressure under existing rules. If the target is killed/incapacitated before communicating, no ordinary callout occurs. This glint does not automatically grant dodge, attack, extra PP, a new reaction, or supernatural vision through solid barriers, nor turn into an area-wide location ping. Underlying magical manufacturing/slots/MP costs remain unspecified.

**Supersession and preservation:** Earlier `main` entries marking stability/attachment tradeoffs and advanced-sight drawbacks unresolved are superseded by this explicit approved *qualitative* specification. Existing DEX100 / paid Aim / Far–Extreme -1 range penalty, no extension beyond Extreme, cover, firearm recoil, shot Power, and handling retain precedence. Specific advanced sight cost, fit time, craftsmanship materials, exact durability, dynamic-stabilization models, target sensory edge cases, and magical-glint origins remain open. Historical exploratory notes remain in files.

**Owners:** `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md` (aim disruption/timing and glint), `27_Ranged_Weapon_Catalogue_DRAFT.md` (attachment and sniper tradeoff), `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` (installation/expense), `10_Canon_Changelog.md` and `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.

## November 11, 2026 — Advanced precision sight Option A approved

**Author expressly approves Option A as working calibration:** A weapon-compatible **advanced precision sight** grants **one category of relief specifically from the Far or Extreme range penalty** on a **genuinely aimed, valid shot**, provided the shooter has **effective DEX ≥100** and a **stable/braced shooting position**. Normal Aim still costs an attack opportunity and grants its approved one-category improvement; the sight's relief is additional, reduces only the range penalty (minimum zero), and creates **no Pressure Points**. Effective/Close gain no extra optic modifier, range boundaries in feet are unchanged, and no beyond-Extreme eligibility is added. A qualified sight does not affect shot Power, armor bypass, cover, recoil, weapon-legal targeting, or the basic mechanical support requirements.

The alternative **Option B 25% range extension remains unapproved**, not cumulative. The advanced sight does not canonize unrelated proposed DEX precision feats, item prices, optic mass/Quality reductions, sight attachment compatibility for every weapon, visibility tricks, or new Aim persistence rules. The distinct firearm Concentrated Burst 10-meter Point Blank rule remains untouched.

## November 11, 2026 — Ranged Power progression by weapon design (author approved)

Build late-game ranged Power around distinct, higher-rated weapon designs. Give advanced bows, crossbows, and firearms their own Power ratings, handling requirements, and firing or reload tradeoffs. Do not introduce a shared ranged Quality multiplier as the mechanism for this progression. Entry models keep their own ratings when an advanced model is introduced. Bow Power remains subject to the approved quadratic partial-draw rule. Crossbow and firearm shot Power still derives from the weapon and its projectile or load. Keep the approved opening-hit emphasis for slower weapons. Exact advanced ratings, requirements, reloads, availability, materials, and ammunition remain under calibration.

Recorded in Equipment and Economy, the ranged catalogue, and the Canon Index.

---

## November 11, 2026 — Ranged draw, reload, and opening-hit benchmarks approved

**Bow draw.** For a shot the archer can legally make, usable draw is the lower of effective STR and the engineered draw cap. Shot Power is the bow-and-arrow full-draw Power multiplied by `(usable draw / draw cap)^2`. DEX governs aim and handling. Individual full-draw Power, draw caps, and minimum controllable partial draw remain open for item calibration.

**Reload pace.** Use one reload turn for most handheld profiles in the working comparison and two for the arbalest. A loaded shot takes its normal attack action. Time spent reloading is separate. Ordinary bow nocking and drawing use the existing bow procedure. Preserve the approved full-turn single-shot pistol and high-capacity magazine reloads. Individual capacity, cycling exceptions, and mounted or siege crew cycles remain subject to their own profiles.

**Damage target.** Give slower ranged weapons a stronger opening hit. Tune the stronger first hit and the full firing cycle together against unarmored, Medium-armored, and Heavy-armored targets. Equal sustained injury output is not the design target. This approval establishes the calibration method and pacing, not the menu's individual shot Power, ammunition, range, or mount test values.

Recorded in Equipment and Economy, Combat, the ranged catalogue, and the Canon Index.

## November 11, 2026 — Melee class, material, and handling benchmarks approved

**Approved working melee calibration.** Reference F for Light / Medium / Heavy is **1.00 / 1.15 / 1.45**. Use the calibrated individual profile where one exists. These class references are not extra multipliers on a whole-profile F. Apply material and workmanship to the applicable attack F once.

Weapon material F factors are Boughsteel **×1.00** across classes, Trufula **×0.95** across classes, and Adamantite **×1.15 / ×1.20 / ×1.25** for Light / Medium / Heavy. Light Adamantite and cross-class Trufula remain working values for later item-specific tuning. Heavy Adamantite stays at ×1.25 for this calibration. Weapon material factors do not modify armor AR.

Keep the catalogue's replacement test minima and material STR/support factors **×1.00 / ×0.50 / ×2.50** for Boughsteel / Trufula / Adamantite. Apply workmanship and round the resulting requirement up once. Do not apply the material STR factor to DEX. These are working benchmarks, with individual profiles and requirements still subject to later item calibration. Carry the lance's supported charge into mounted combat. Continue with the [ranged catalogue](27_Ranged_Weapon_Catalogue_DRAFT.md).

Updated Equipment and Economy, both melee drafts, and the Canon Index.

## November 11, 2026 — Unified Piercing bypass and secondary thrust ratings (author approved)

All genuine Piercing attacks use **50% base AR bypass**, including eligible secondary thrusts. A secondary blade-tip thrust uses **×0.82 of the weapon's adjusted primary F**. A purpose-built secondary spike or beak uses **×0.90 of primary F**. Apply the factor once, with the attack's applicable quality and material factors. The actual blade tip, spike, or beak must support the attack. An earned rear hit can raise Piercing bypass to 60% under the existing rear rule.

The greatsword's Point thrust uses ×0.82 of its adjusted Slashing primary F, equivalent to relative form F0.902 before its shared ×0.875 shape factor. This supersedes the former ×0.90 / 25% thrust profile and the earlier calibration goal that the thrust always trail a comparable maul against plate. The maul's primary remains Bludgeoning Wide Sweep at 25% base bypass. Absolute class and material factors remain provisional.

Recorded in Equipment and Economy, Combat, the melee catalogue, and the Canon Index.

## November 11, 2026 — Earned rear-hit armor bypass increased (author approved)

A qualifying rear hit adds **10 percentage points** to applicable armor bypass, capped at 100%. Apply the resulting bypass to covered AR before squaring its contribution to Defense Power. A base 50% attack therefore reaches 60%. Surprise or a tactically earned rear opening is still required, including appropriate meaningful Pressure when it actually exposes the rear. Merely standing behind an alert opponent does not qualify. This supersedes the former 5-point bonus. The separate specialized Piercing profile remains retired.

Updated Combat, Equipment and Economy, and the Canon Index.

## November 11, 2026 — Melee alternate attacks and catalogue alignment

**Approved working calibration.** Start genuine secondary attacks at ×0.90 of primary F, with specific profiles taking precedence. Improvised pommel and butt strikes remain capped at comparable unarmed Power. The reference Boughsteel poleaxe's built-in axe face uses F1.089, Slashing Sweep, and 0% AR bypass. Its built-in hammer face uses F1.089, Bludgeoning Narrow, and 25% AR bypass. Apply quality and applicable material factors once. These faces require the actual head to be present. The former axe test candidate was F1.134.

**Catalogue decisions.** The dagger supports a Point thrust and a Narrow slash. The stiletto is thrust-only. The standard mace uses Bludgeoning Narrow. A mace needs a purpose-built spike for a secondary Piercing attack; flanges alone do not qualify. A warhammer's Piercing alternate requires a beak. The war pick's point remains its primary. The halberd's secondary Point uses its top spike; a glaive can thrust with a suitable blade tip. The maul uses the previously approved Bludgeoning Wide Sweep and its clearance rule. Carry the other primary ratings forward at their existing test or draft status. Evaluate the lance's ordinary thrust with its supported charge when the charge procedure is ready.

**Resolved by the subsequent Piercing ruling above.** Review secondary Piercing F and AR bypass together before changing those profiles. The currently approved greatsword thrust still uses ×0.90 of its adjusted primary F and 25% bypass.

Updated Equipment and Economy, the melee catalogue, and the Canon Index. The older maul row incorrectly showed Sweep.

## November 11, 2026 — Squad formation and coverage Power (author approved)

For abstract squad resolution, count only the distinct members the attack can actually threaten from its current position. Apply the existing coverage multiplier to Line as well as Sweep and Wide Sweep. One, two, and three eligible members give ×1.00, ×1.50, and ×2.00 aggregate Power, respectively. Sweep caps at two and Wide Sweep and Line cap at three. Apply the relevant facing, clearance, reach, alignment, and obstruction rules before counting. If there are no eligible members, there is no legal attack. Individual hits use normal Power.

Squad maneuvering changes the count. A spread-out squad may give a Sweep only one reachable member. A Sweep from the very end of a file can likewise catch only the end member if the rest lie beyond the usable arc or reach. Count the actual geometry for each attack.

**Cogmarshal follow-up.** Revisit how squads arrange themselves and change formation during the Cogmarshal pass. Formation choices, orders, timing, and costs are open.

Updated Combat, Equipment and Economy, the melee catalogue, the Canon Index, and Centaurs and Smithcraft.

## November 11, 2026 — Sweep clearance in confined spaces (author approved)

**Minimum maneuvering room.** A Sweep requires enough clear room for two people to stand shoulder to shoulder. A Wide Sweep requires enough for three. These are minimums for making the attack, even against one target. The actual weapon motion and its path must also be clear.

**Weapon coverage.** A slashing attack uses its listed coverage. A player cannot downgrade a Sweep or Wide Sweep to a single-target slash to fit a confined space. A light slashing cleaver with a compact motion can work in cramped quarters. Light handling alone does not waive the clearance requirement of an attack listed as Sweep. A weapon can use a genuine alternate attack if its own motion fits. A greatsword whose Wide Sweep is blocked can use its single-target Point thrust if there is room to thrust.

**Terrain.** Judge clearance after accounting for physical barriers such as an ice wall. A barrier can deny room to sweep without directly targeting a combatant. A pike's Line uses its approved straight-path and reach rules. In a tight corridor it can hit two aligned targets when both are reachable. The benchmark uses three greatsword hits in open ground, one greatsword thrust in a tight corridor, and two pike hits in the corridor as scenario assumptions. Individual hits use normal Power. The squad rule above now governs Line.

Recorded in Combat, Equipment and Economy, the melee catalogue, and the Canon Index. Existing forward arcs, target caps, and Line individual-target resolution still apply.

## November 11, 2026 — Line individual-target resolution approved

The author approved using Sweep's individual-target procedure for Line. Use Sweep's procedure to resolve each eligible individual target in the same attack action. Each successful pike hit uses its normal Power and 50% AR bypass against that target's own Defense. A front enemy or shield that physically blocks access makes a farther target ineligible. Missing the front enemy does not by itself end the attack. Alignment and physical access govern execution under existing combat rules. Apply no special Line difficulty modifier or reduction in Power for later targets. The squad rule above now governs Line. Penetration Proficiencies remain unresolved. This resolves the earlier open questions about individual difficulty and Power. Updated Combat, Equipment and Economy, the melee catalogue, and the Canon Index.

**Line coverage.** One Line attack may damage up to three distinct, physically reachable targets positioned in a straight line outward from the wielder. Line should be harder to execute and somewhat weaker than Sweep. Sweep retains its two-target cap in the forward arc. Individual difficulty and Power were resolved in the subsequent approval above. The squad rule above now governs Line. Any penetration Proficiency remains unresolved.

**Pike and primary Piercing.** Retire the separate specialized Piercing profile (F0.85 and 65% AR bypass). The Heavy, Long, Both-stat pike uses ordinary primary Piercing (relative F0.90 and 50% AR bypass) with a pike-specific ×0.90 shape factor applied once. Its combined relative F is 0.81 before class, material and quality. When qualified, use A = 0.65 × (effective STR + effective DEX). Reference test minima are STR14 and DEX11. Final requirements and absolute class, material and individual weapon Fs remain under calibration.

## November 10, 2026 — Celestite Leydrain tiers, additive overlaps, voluntary-combat Soul Shred and no zero-MP extra punishment (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**LATEST SPECIFIC APPROVAL OVERRIDES EARLIER CELESTITE LEYDRAIN <5 AT ALL ARMOR CLASSES:** Celestite armor's innate Leydrain **radius and strict spell-channel cancellation threshold** scale by protection class: **Light: radius 5m, nullifies channeled spells costing strictly LESS THAN 5 MP; Medium: 10m, suppresses spells costing strictly LESS THAN 10 MP; Heavy: 15m, suppresses spells costing strictly LESS THAN 15 MP.** A spell whose relevant MP cost equals the threshold is NOT canceled by that field's under-threshold rule alone. Friendly/beneficial/self-originated spells remain subject to cancellation. The existing separate automatic **wearer MP drain of 5/10/15 MP per turn** for Light/Medium/Heavy is UNCHANGED. This change is to the spell-canceling numeric threshold, not the radius or armor MP debit.

**AUTHOR APPROVES LEYDRAIN STACKING:** Multiple simultaneously active Celestite Leydrain effects, including armor+weapon or other valid Celestite items, do **not** merely pick the strongest; they combine to make heavily equipped Celestite users especially difficult to affect magically. **Provisional operational interpretation for straightforward field interaction (pending explicit author confirmation if necessary):** At a location inside multiple field radii, **add all those fields' applicable spell-suppression MP thresholds**, giving an effective strictly-below combined cutoff. E.g. within the intersection of two Heavy-armor-equivalent 15-MP fields, channeling of a spell costing LESS THAN 30 MP vanishes; outside the intersection but in only one 15-MP field, the single 15 cutoff applies. Radii represent distinct areas and do NOT automatically add their linear distances. The weapon's own numerical Leydrain threshold/radius, if not yet specified by its subject canon, remain OPEN and cannot be fabricated. This additive-threshold scheme is an implementation proposal consistent with the author's explicit stacking directive, rather than an invented previously approved weapon value.

**AUTHOR APPROVES 'SOUL SHRED' NAME AND VOLUNTARY COMBAT CONDITION:** The armor's fixed-reference accumulated-XP erosion is officially named **Soul Shred**. Celestite armor does NOT shred XP merely while worn, during travel, in passive proximity to monsters, or solely because hostile creatures initiate combat/attack the wearer. It activates in **actual combat WHEN THE WEARER CHOOSES TO FIGHT BACK / WILLINGLY ENGAGE in the conflict**, taking deliberate combat participation rather than passively being attacked as the trigger. Thereafter while actively committed to the combat engagement, **each qualifying turn** destroys **5% of the wearer's TOTAL XP snapshot taken when they first donned the armor**, with the original fixed baseline, possibly causing level regression and associated loss. Do not charge one Soul Shred turn for every ambient enemy projectile or automatically from initiative. An ineffective kobold harassing a nonretaliating Celestite traveler does not activate Soul Shred. Do not interpret the rule as allowing attack one turn then negate the cost every other turn simply by holding a defensive posture within the same continuing voluntary fight; detailed engagement-end/disengagement boundaries can be settled in combat rules. Nonviolent travel, clear nonengagement and actual withdrawal do not by themselves trigger Soul Shred. The weapon's distinct already approved XP/soul loss per stroke still stacks when wielded/used; no free exemption for actively wielding both.

**NO EXTRA FATE AT ZERO MANA (APPROVED):** Celestite armor MP drain reduces currently available MP no lower than **0 MP**. If MP reaches zero or is insufficient to cover the full listed debit, the wearer simply has zero MP, with no separate soul injury, instant incapacitation, wound, XP penalty, spell explosion or forced end of armor functionality merely because the MP debit could not be fully paid. Soul Shred and anti-magic field retain their otherwise legitimate triggers/effects; normal ordinary consequences of being unable to pay MP for one's own spells remain. The armor's special exemption from stat-shortfall due to its own induced level-down remains intact.

**UNRESOLVED EDGE CASES:** Detailed exact combat commitment trigger for purely defensive fighting, duration of an engagement or disengagement, once-per-turn scheduling and rounding, persistent original XP snapshot per wearer through removal/re-donning, how canceled spells are treated when channeled outside and entering fields, specific Celestite weapon Leydrain numeric parameters and total item stacking, and precise QV AR/MR/STR/DEX/SPD profiles (prior Quill suggested but not approved Heavy AR180/MR185/STR65). Do not invent Celestite weapon item data or distort existing godslayer per-stroke soul erosion. No XP drain should occur just from an imposed encounter with no voluntary retaliation/engagement.


## November 10, 2026 — Thornspring turn-long offensive/evasive Springload; Celestite Quality V armor approved (LATEST AUTHOR DECISION)

### Thornspring burst timing correction (APPROVED)

**SUPERSEDES one-maneuver-only duration:** When a Thornspring wearer deliberately **expends one Springload charge**, the class-specific **situational SPD bonus lasts for the ENTIRE CURRENT TURN**, not one movement, dash or dodge. This applies to applicable **offensive and evasive SPD-based combat comparisons**, including sudden charges/closing on an opponent and running away from incoming attacks where normal rules permit. Quality V working class bonuses are **Light +50%, Medium +75%, Heavy +35%** to effective situational SPD for that turn, not passive permanent SPD or an extra action. It is not itself a generic multiplicative bonus to weapon Power, an additional attack, a free hit, or an override of Trapped/otherwise illegal dodges. Existing wound, injury, equipment-shortfall, cover, pressure, reaction, action/initiative rules remain unless a separately approved rule says otherwise. The mechanism's exact applicability to attacks beyond positional/relative-speed comparison and turn-end timing remain integration details, not permission to change universal attack Power. Springload capacity one, permanent charge storage (including charges gained in combat), full-turn manual winding by wearer/ally, and genuine-hit recharge remain approved.

### Celestite divine armor (APPROVED)

**EXCEPTIONAL EXISTENCE AND AVAILABILITY:** Celestite can be forged as **Light, Medium and Heavy armor**, but **ONLY at Smithcraft/workmanship Quality V**, with NO Quality I, II, III or IV item versions or corresponding material stats. As with Celestite weapons, actual production still requires the original exceptional **triune divine forging with Arrest, Surrender and Baphomet** and extraordinary Centaur craftsmanship, not merely an ordinary Quality V smith or market purchase. This is an approved extraordinary exception to normal material tiers. Preserve the existing Celestite anti-ley-line, soul-destroying God War lore and unconditional Drakyn treasure-detection consequences.

**PHYSICAL/MAGICAL PROTECTION AND REQUIREMENTS:** At matching protective armor class, coverage and Quality V, Celestite's baseline **Armor Resistance (AR) is slightly HIGHER than Adamantite**, and its baseline **Magic Resistance (MR) slightly HIGHER than Nullporcelain**, not astronomically beyond either. Its required **STR lies between Boughsteel's and Adamantite's corresponding Quality V armor support STR**. Actual Quality V Full Heavy comparison anchors: Adamantite AR170 (working approved), Nullporcelain MR170 (working approved), Boughsteel STR36 and Adamantite STR91 (working test/support benchmarks). Exact Celestite AR/MR/STR values and appropriate other-class comparative Adamantite AR/Boughsteel/Adamantite STR and mobilities are OPEN, not numerical canon. No automatic celestial extra armor penetration, regeneration, immunity or universal MR-to-AR equation is invented.

**PASSIVE MP DRAIN, EVERY TURN WHILE WORN:** Celestite Light armor drains the wearer **5 MP per turn**; Medium **10 MP per turn**; Heavy **15 MP per turn**. This is intrinsic anti-magic/soul effect, not optional equipment enchantment expenditure. Do not mislabel it as current equipment Soul Strain. Timing of debit and behavior when the wearer has fewer MP than required remain open.

**LEYDRAIN (APPROVED):** Celestite's passive anti-spell field has radius **5 meters Light**, **10 meters Medium**, **15 meters Heavy** from the wearer. **Any spell being channeled using LESS THAN 5 MP within that field vanishes**, including wearer-authored, friendly, healing and other positive spells, not merely hostile spells. A spell channeled with exactly 5 MP does not meet this specific below-five threshold (but is not thereby automatically immune to other Celestite defenses or effects); no new blanket cancellation of high-MP spells from this numeric rule alone. Distinguish channeled spells from unrelated nonspell environmental effects until canon defines their interactions. Whether the mana count refers to single activation cost versus total channel expenditure, overlap resolution when multiple Celestite items are present, and spell created outside the radius entering the field are implementation questions, not authorization to change the approved threshold/radii.

**SOUL/EXPERIENCE DESTRUCTION WHILE IN BATTLE (APPROVED):** On the FIRST DONNING of a Celestite armor ensemble, snapshot **the wearer's TOTAL accumulated XP** as the armor's fixed erosion reference amount (do not recompute 5% from declining current XP each turn). During battle, **each turn the wearer loses 5% OF THAT RECORDED TOTAL XP**, potentially losing levels and associated normal level-derived stats/skills, matching existing Celestite direct soul-erosion lore rather than refundable Soul Strain. XP loss is a fixed baseline percentage per turn, not 5% of *remaining* XP. The author's rule specifically states **in battle**; behavior on repeated doffing/redonning, survival at XP zero, rounding fractional XP, end-of-turn ordering, and soul destruction's terminal state need careful integration. No XP recovery or moral-protection exemption is granted by armor. Wearing Celestite after leveling down **does not incur any equipment-stat-shortfall penalty caused solely by reduced STR/DEX from this Celestite XP erosion**; after it is properly put on, the armor remains operable even if the bearer can no longer satisfy the original stat requirements. This exception does not grant general stat bonuses, eligibility to newly don armor without qualifications, immunity to wounds, or exemption from unrelated equipment shortfalls. Treat other new equipment or unrelated requirements under existing rules.

**STACKING WITH CELESTITE WEAPON (APPROVED):** If the wearer simultaneously wields a Celestite weapon, the weapon's existing **MP drain (if any applicable from the weapon's own canon), XP/soul drain per stroke, and anti-ley-line/Leydrain capability** apply **IN ADDITION** to armor's passive MP drain, battle-per-turn XP erosion and Leydrain. They do not cancel or merge for a discount. This is an additive danger and stacking design directive; exact numerical weapon MP drain and field threshold/radius stack resolution have **NOT** been separately provided in the existing weapon subject canon and must NOT be invented. Existing approved weapon soul-erosion per stroke and False Apotheosis/conviction peril are retained. Any two anti-magic field overlap effects should be respected rather than silently selecting only the better source; exact additive distance/threshold formula is not approved.

**HISTORICAL CELESTITE PRICE REFERENCE (SUPERSEDED NOVEMBER 12, 2026):** For a comparable armor class, coverage and Quality V item, Celestite armor's **LISTED DISPLAY PRICE is 5× the nominal Adamantite armor price**, marked with an asterisk **'*'** immediately clarifying that **Celestite CANNOT BE BOUGHT** and its exceptional Centaur + triune divine crafting requirements MUST be satisfied before such an artifact can be forged. This is a comparative reference valuation only, not an actual Gem market sale, conventional crafting recipe, stock availability or evidence of ordinary buyback economics. No numeric Adamantite armor Gem price is fabricated by this ruling.

**OPEN FOLLOW-UP:** finalize Quality V armor AR/MR/STR/DEX/SPD benchmarks, exact MP debit and depleted-pool result, first-donning XP-snapshot persistence if item is transferred, XP rounding/floor and death/soul-zero effects, details of how overlapping weapon+armor Leydrain radii/MP cutoff behave, whether MP limits affect active channeling across the field boundary, and whether an experience-loss event also impacts non-level XP accounting. No redefinition of the other eleven pure materials or author-approved Celestite weapon effects.


## November 10, 2026 — Thornspring numerical burst benchmarks approved; stable permanent charges and construct engineering role (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**EXPLICIT AUTHOR ACCEPTANCE:** Author approves Quill's immediately preceding Thornspring recommendations INCLUDING the previously proposed **one Springload charge capacity per ordinary suit**, class-specific one-maneuver **Light +50%, Medium +75%, Heavy +35% situational effective SPD bursts**, and the provisional **Quality V Full-coverage armor profiles Light AR25/STR8/DEX16/MR0, Medium AR55/STR12/DEX26/MR0, Heavy AR90/STR15/DEX36/MR0** as **approved working numerical calibration**. Armor is intentionally below corresponding Boughsteel physical protection, with low STR and unusually high DEX requirements; no passive speed increase, generic extra action/dodge, bypass of Trapped or use of SPD for unrelated attack Power. The burst applies only to the qualifying dodge/evasive movement maneuver and expends its one charge; preserves existing action economy and quality-reduced gear requirements under global rules. The working suggested **physical-hit recharge criterion about 70% of a Quality-matched generalist's Solid-wound Power threshold** is accepted as a provisional balancing target, NOT a newly resolved universal combat threshold or spell damage formula. Incoming genuine physical contact can replenish this charge if qualifying, even when armor prevented injury; the gained charge does not retroactively improve the generating attack's own defense. Exact reference-generalist builds and per-quality thresholds remain pending.

**AUTHOR'S IMPORTANT EXPLICIT CAVEAT:** Charges acquired from *incoming attacks in combat* **DO NOT DISAPPEAR** afterward. Thornspring is stable and efficient; **ALL Springload charges, whether acquired from winding or physical impacts and whether acquired in combat or beforehand, remain stored indefinitely until deliberately released/spent**, within the suit's one-charge normal capacity. No expiration at end of turn, at encounter boundary, due to injury, or after ordinary calm movement. Do not reintroduce earlier ephemeral-charge suggestion. A wearer or ally can spend one full turn operating built-in winding gear to refill it to full, including before an engagement, and need not suffer attacks to start ready. Repeated meaningful impacts can replenish charge **after it has been used**; additional impacts while fully charged do not grant extra charges or stacking speed multipliers. Injury is not a separate automatic recharge trigger; genuine force transferred through the armor is the mechanism, and being wounded under intense pressure is one dramatic way this can happen.

**CONSTRUCT ENGINEERING WORLD LORE APPROVED:** Thornspring's reliable, unusually efficient elastic mechanical energy storage/recoil management is a **major foundational material/engineering backbone of Mystarian constructs and clockwork mechanisms**. Appropriate construct designs may use Thornspring in recoil assemblies, springs, joints, energy return, locomotion, shock recovery or actuators, subject to their individual build's material/craft limits. This is substantive lore supporting the broader clockwork industry; it does **NOT** mean all constructs must be made of it, every construct gains Thornspring armor's +SPD or charge mechanic, every unit receives a free stat increase, or the material creates energy without input. Existing clockwork craft, Leywell, Cogsong and stat rules remain unchanged absent separate approval.

**STILL OPEN:** Quality I–IV Thornspring AR, STR/DEX and movement effects, exact percent/damage benchmark implementation after building reference generalists, class-specific burst movement reach/route, integration with reaction/initiative and any construct-specific designs/stat bonuses. The one-charge/three-class Quality V profiles are current working canon, subject to future balance corrections. Glassweave has already received its pure-material concept and breakage treatment; among the named twelve materials Celestite is the outstanding extraordinary/divine exception to ordinary material armor calibration. Advanced composites remain separate follow-up from pure-material identities and must not silently erase past design decisions.


## November 10, 2026 — Thornspring stored readiness, manual winding and impact recharge (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**APPROVED THORNSPRING SPRINGLOAD POLICY:** Thornspring armor has a physical **winding gear**. **Either the wearer or a willing ally can spend one full turn activating/operating it to restore its currently available Springload charge capacity to FULL** (i.e., fill charge slots, not increase their maximum count). A suitably prepared suit can enter combat fully wound; no repeated jumps, staged falls, ally attacks, fake-threat gymnastics, or other contrived antics are required. Stored Springload **does NOT expire at turn end or after a fixed number of turns**; it **remains until deliberately spent/released**. Preserving opening-turn readiness is an explicit author design objective. An ally investing their turn in a combat rewind is a meaningful tactical choice, subject to ordinary access/position/action constraints. There is no automatic free extra attack, dodge, or turn from winding.

**APPROVED SECONDARY RECHARGE ROUTE:** In combat, **actually being struck by a meaningful physical impact** can restore Springload without operating the winding gear. Reuse the **quality-matched reference-generalist wounding benchmark / relative impact threshold philosophy discussed for Hearthiron's fire recharge**, translated into appropriate physical-impact Power; do NOT require the wearer to take an actual wound to benefit, and do NOT reuse Bellcopper's distinct actual-post-defense-wound charging trigger. The impact must genuinely contact and load the springs; trivial harmless taps, staged motions, attack attempts that fully miss, psychic effects and energy-only attacks without physical impact do not qualify. The recharge cannot retroactively alter defense against the strike which generated it. No extra charging from routine noncombat motion.

**AUTHOR-APPROVED THORNSPRING SPECIALTY DIRECTION FROM CURRENT CONVERSATION:** This material should grant **brief powerful SPD bursts rather than passive SPD bonuses**, optimizing dodges/evasive positional defense; especially **Medium** should gain a large situational SPD boost on releasing Springload. Its normal physical AR is **lower than Boughsteel but not negligible**; **intrinsic MR0**. It has **low STR support requirement near conventional Boughsteel DEX handling requirement** and unusually high DEX handling requirement near Boughsteel's STR requirement at corresponding class/quality, since controlled elastic acceleration is skill-intensive. Existing global armor/gear shortfall rules apply. Do NOT invent a generic static passive SPD increase or free automatic dodge, nor allow burst to bypass actual Trapped/no-dodge conditions, create attacks, change initiative retroactively or affect unrelated weapon Power.

**NOT YET APPROVED NUMERIC PARAMETERS:** Earlier Quill proposed full-coverage Quality V Thornspring **Light AR25/STR8/DEX16, Medium AR55/STR12/DEX26, Heavy AR90/STR15/DEX36; intrinsic MR0 for all**, and Springload **one-charge** burst SPD bonuses of **Light +50%, Medium +75%, Heavy +35%**. These specific AR/STR/DEX and +SPD percentages are **unapproved placeholders**, not numeric canon. The proposed **70% of quality-matched reference generalist's Solid-wound Power threshold** as physical impact recharge is also still an **unapproved quantitative suggestion**; the benchmark should not be applied using a spell/elemental MR formula as if it were finalized physical Power. Exact charge-capacity by class, gain-per-hit, overcharging behavior, effects of shield-only hits, whether emergency ally winding needs a specific position, and timing of actual dodge spending are pending. Preserve earlier canon Thornspring extraordinary elasticity and recoil management.


## November 9, 2026 — Hearthiron low-output warmth preserves combat charges (LATEST EXPLICIT AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**AUTHOR APPROVES QUILL'S MOST RECENT RECOMMENDATION:** Hearthiron armor, including **Medium and Heavy**, can provide sustained **ordinary low-output radiant warmth without consuming a discrete Furnace Burst combat charge** while the armor has usable background retained heat. This is an intentionally negligible long-duration draw relative to a battle-scale offensive thermal discharge, NOT an exemption from finite heat storage forever and NOT permission to provide unlimited large-area extreme heating. **Medium Furnace Burst remains one charge; Heavy remains two**, each at equal Quality-based damage. Offensive burst costs a charge, high-output active effects meaningfully spend heat, extreme heat/pyromancy can replenish in combat, campfire replenishment is slower and unspecified, and high-output thermal leakage remains dangerous. Light remains the personal warmth/survival specialist. Exact AoE radius, elemental attack Power and Quality damage benchmarks, 70%-of-generalist threshold recharge test, action cost, and full resource conversion are **not newly canonized by this approval**; the previous assistant explicitly called them proposals and the author has not selected their final values. Next discussion target: **Thornspring**, canonically highly elastic and recoil managing, but proposed armor specialties still require design approval.


## November 9, 2026 — Hearthiron Medium Furnace Burst shares Heavy damage with smaller reservoir (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**APPROVED CLASS IDENTITY AND CHARGE COUNTS:** **Medium Hearthiron armor HAS Furnace Burst**, an actual offensive area thermal discharge **of the SAME base Power/damage as the Heavy Hearthiron Furnace Burst at the SAME workmanship Quality**. The two are distinguished by stored uses: **Medium holds ONE Furnace Burst charge**, **Heavy holds TWO Furnace Burst charges** between full replenishments. Medium retains its approved ability to regulate shared radiant warmth, while Heavy retains more stored fuel and strong furnace/contact-heat potential. Medium is not reduced to a glorified lantern; do NOT assign it a weaker burst multiplier solely for being Medium. Light retains its personal survival/thermal comfort identity, with no Furnace Burst established for Light. The author's expectation is that Medium armor commonly combines features associated with Light and Heavy. In this case the difference is endurance/capacity rather than single-burst damage.

**ALREADY APPROVED RECHARGE PHILOSOPHY:** More intense external fire, suitable pyromancy, and sufficiently strong enemy attacks may recharge Hearthiron in battle; ordinary campfire heat needs an unspecified longer time. Thermal exhaustion turns off charged offensive functionality but does not lower base AR or x3 fire resistance. Thermal discharge endangers exposed bystanders and environment. A Furnace Burst expends one stored charge; it is not an extra free attack on top of ordinary action economy and is not a wielder-STR/DEX/INT-scaled weapon attack. Attacks are Quality-oriented rather than attacker-stat-oriented as the author has directed.

**STILL ONLY QUILL'S UNAPPROVED PLACEHOLDER NUMERIC TESTS:** Earlier suggestions to use **3m AoE radius**, **one attack action**, **medium weapon/longsword-equivalent magnitude** with hypothetical **F1.10** and reference Quality I–V attributes **25/35/50/65/80** (producing draft Power **756/1482/3025/5112/7744**), and rapid recharge at approximately **70% of a corresponding reference-generalist's wounding thermal threshold**, are BALANCING PROPOSALS, not a finalized elemental-damage formula or canon numerics. The author has explicitly endorsed **equal Medium/Heavy damage and ONE/TWO charges**, not yet ratified the preceding entire table, radius or recharge percentage. Preserve earlier design goals for AoE thermal damage comparable with a Medium weapon and scaling from equipment Quality rather than attacker's stats. **Elemental Magnitude resolution remains unfinished**; do not incorrectly apply the physical sword Power equation automatically to fire damage, or treat STR/DEX or attack frequency as its scaling mechanism.


## November 8, 2026 — Hearthiron thermal exhaustion and hazardous heat discharge APPROVED; ordinary ice weakness superseded (LATEST AUTHOR REVISION)

**LATEST AUTHOR DIRECTION REPLACES THE PREVIOUS ELEMENTAL-DRAWBACK PROPOSAL:** Preserve Hearthiron's approved **×3 Fire resistance**, high thermal inertia, substantial Bellcopper-like weight and Reprise Bronze-like physical AR. **Retire the blanket ×1.5 ICE VULNERABILITY** from the previous Hearthiron proposal; it is **SUPERSEDED** by the author's preference for heat-resource exhaustion and hazardous heat release as meaningful drawbacks. This does **not** create any new numeric ice resistance or immunity; ordinary ice-magic damage still uses applicable rules and temperature-change resistance is a separate material property. The old ice multiplier appears in historical sections below solely for archival context.

**APPROVED HEAT AS FINITE RESOURCE:** Hearthiron stores heat and consumes that store primarily when the wearer deliberately uses its most active thermal abilities (notably Heavy's offensive furnace/contact heat and extended strong radiation, with intensity governing expenditure). At low passive warmth output, its exceptionally high storage retains heat over long periods; it does NOT lose combat-scale charges merely by existing. **Thermal exhaustion** reduces/disables heat-powered active abilities until replenished, without removing basic physical AR or inherently changing its approved baseline elemental Fire resistance. High-output heat use is a tactical choice with an actual opportunity cost, not unlimited free continuous fire damage.

**APPROVED HAZARDOUS LEAKAGE:** The wearer can regulate warmth and use proper insulation, but deliberate high-heat output can endanger nearby friends, carried gear, flammable environments, and creatures in direct contact. Use existing relevant fire/elemental injury, status, reach and action rules once fully specified; no automatically free attack or guaranteed burning through defenses. Consequences of exposing others to the heat are part of the material's downside.

**APPROVED DYNAMIC REHEATING, REJECTS SLOW-FORCED-RECHARGE DRAWBACK:** Extremely powerful external heat or suitably potent pyromancy can rapidly replenish Hearthiron **even during battle**, and opponents' sufficiently powerful fire effects may be exploited to refill it. **Ordinary campfires do not instantly restore useful combat reserves**; they replenish slowly over a deliberately **unspecified longer period**, with no arbitrary numeric hours or recharge turns canonized. Refilling depends on external intensity/actual available heat, not free magical MP generation from armor. Incoming fire remains a threat/effect according to the normal resistance framework; fuel recovery is not automatic universal immunity or unlimited stored capacity. The author rejected 'slow temperature adjustment' as a universal gameplay drawback, while high thermal inertia remains an inherent material property and intense heat explicitly overcomes practical recharge delays.

**UNRESOLVED (DO NOT INVENT NUMBERS):** heat reservoir capacity and units, active-ability costs, heat gain per spell/attack/ambient source, exactly when active fire damage applies, range, how fast extreme heat recharges, ordinary campfire time, interaction of generated heat with spell mana, whether capacity changes with quality/class, AR I–IV, MR, STR/SPD and fire/ice formula implementation. Resume pure-material design with **Thornspring** once the author is satisfied; no change to other materials.


## November 8, 2026 — Hearthiron weighted thermal armor and elemental profile APPROVED (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**HEARTHIRON AUTHOR-APPROVED MATERIAL/ARMOR IDENTITY:** Pure Hearthiron armor has **roughly the mass/burden of Bellcopper** at corresponding armor class, quality and coverage (both are unusually heavy, including Light class); its normal **physical AR should be on par with Reprise Bronze** by comparable quality/class. Use already-approved **Quality V Reprise Bronze** AR reference by protective class **Light30, Medium60, Heavy100** as the **working corresponding Hearthiron physical AR calibration**; this is not approval of a comprehensive Quality I–IV Hearthiron ladder or the still-open exact STR/SPD burdens. Its unusually high thermal inertia means it is **difficult to change the metal's temperature** in either direction: Hearthiron stores hot temperatures, gives off light/heat over years at modest demand, and can retain useful thermal conditions under harsh climates. It is NOT an infinite energy source; high heat output draws down stored heat faster. Standard proper armor insulation protects the wearer from the metal itself.

**AUTHOR APPROVES ELEMENTAL RELATIONSHIPS:** Hearthiron offers **×3 Fire RESISTANCE** and **×1.5 ICE VULNERABILITY**. Keep these distinct from ordinary AR/MR and physical armor penetration. The appropriate exact Elemental Magnitude/resistance formula placement remains unresolved; do NOT interpret ×3 fire resistance as '3× fire damage' or as a 3× AR multiplier, nor treat the ice vulnerability as invalidating its strong ordinary cold-weather insulation. Environmental cold preservation and acute magical ice vulnerability coexist as specified: magical/elemental ice is the weakness; harsh weather warmth is its specialty.

**AUTHOR APPROVES PRIOR ASSISTANT HEARTHIRON ARMOR CLASS DIRECTIONS:** **Light** armor supplies wearer comfort, persistent warmth and exceptional cold-weather survival. **Medium** can regulate and radiate heat to assist nearby companions. **Heavy** provides a more substantial stored-heat bank capable of **controllable dangerous radiant heat and contact burns in grapples/close-combat**, with built-in safe insulation for the wearer, not an arbitrary free attack action. User also approves the idea of controllable heat output including passive insulated/protective, radiant warming and high-output furnace-style applications; these are **design directions**, NOT new spell/status damage or exact activation actions. Any contact-burn/radiant injury must pass through appropriate existing fire/elemental combat rules; do not infer free harm through all physical protection, auto-ignite everything, or grant new attacks outside normal action economy.

**OPEN NUMERIC/PROCEDURAL FOLLOW-UP:** Precise heat storage units, warm/furnace active duration, rate of cooling and recharge, whether and how rapidly the wearer can toggle output, radiance distance and environmental counterplay, Light/Medium/Heavy quality I–IV AR, Hearthiron MR, detailed STR/SPD support and armor handling load, exact placement of fire/ice factors in future Elemental Magnitude formulas. Bellcopper's working burden STR48/−25% at QV Heavy remains a PROPOSAL, so no numerical Hearthiron burden is finalized by the author's 'about as heavy' approval. **NEXT PURE MATERIAL: THORNSPRING**, known for extraordinary elasticity, stored spring force and recoil management (risk: uncontrolled release). Its possible armor applications require proposal/approval rather than assuming existing mechanics.


## November 8, 2026 — Bellcopper reservoir charges ONLY on actual wounds after enhanced Defense (LATEST AUTHOR CORRECTION)

**HIGHEST-PRECEDENCE AUTHOR DECISION SUPERSEDES PRIOR TRIGGER PROPOSAL:** For Heavy Bellcopper (×2.00 final physical Defense Power when empty) and Medium Bellcopper (×1.50 when empty), resolve the relevant **armor-contacting physical attack WITH Bellcopper's enhanced final Defense Power** and all ordinary applicable combat rules. The reservoir begins charging / ceases to be Ready **ONLY IF the attack STILL actually wounds the wearer** (normally Solid-or-worse physical injury after applying the bonus). An intercepted attack that resolves **Superficial and inflicts no wound** DOES NOT charge it, even if that attack would have been Solid-or-worse against unboosted ordinary Defense Power. This explicitly REJECTS the earlier Quill proposal to use *would wound against normal Defense* as its activation criterion. It preserves the reward for successfully neutralizing blows rather than taxing every dangerous-looking hit.

**THREE-TURN RESET RULE:** Once an actual post-defense wound triggers reservoir charging, apply normal, unboosted physical Defense Power to later attacks until the reservoir has drained through three uninterrupted qualifying quiet turns. Meaningful additional armor-contacting impacts can prolong/reset its settling as previously directed, but do not reinterpret a successfully harmless *first* intercepted blow as stored energy. Exact later-hit 'meaningful' eligibility and turn-count bookkeeping can be specified when needed without contradicting the wound-only initial trigger. Keep armor AR equal to Boughsteel, Bellcopper considerably heavier, Light stealth hush/grapple vocal silence and Medium partial hush. The kinetic sound release's quantitative offensive effects, and specific STR/SPD values, remain OPEN. Example with END139 and AR115: normal Defense 32,546; ready Heavy 65,092; attack Power approx. 34,971 resolves superficial at 65,092, therefore **reservoir remains empty/ready**, not Ringing.

**NEXT PURE MATERIAL FOR DESIGN: HEARTHIRON.** Canon Hearthiron can retain red heat and emit light for years under low draw, cools faster under heavier heat demand and needs insulation to avoid burning people or surroundings. It is a heat battery, not infinite energy. Proposed armor direction is a temperature-management utility suit with user-selectable insulation or radiant warmth, without inventing specific fire damage/status/AR/MR/energy rates before the author's approval.


## November 8, 2026 — Bellcopper Boughsteel-equivalent AR, greater mass, final Defense Power resonance (LATEST AUTHOR DECISION)

**LATEST APPROVAL:** At identical Quality, protective Light/Medium/Heavy class and actual coverage, pure Bellcopper has **the same physical AR as Boughsteel**, rather than a higher normal passive AR. Thus the established working Quality I–V **Full Heavy AR ladder is 75/85/95/105/115**. Bellcopper is **significantly heavier and demands more handling STR / has a worse intrinsic mobility load than Boughsteel at equivalent class/quality/coverage**, INCLUDING in its Light configuration. This supersedes any notion that Light Bellcopper must be physically lightweight merely because its protective class is Light. **Exact numeric weight/STR/SPD ladders remain to be author-approved**, including this author's yet-unprovided preference on how large 'significant' is. Low/medium/full class armor retains its own acoustic specialty from previous approvals.

**AUTHOR-SELECTED KINETIC RESONANCE MECHANISM supersedes earlier 'halve incoming Attack Power' assistant proposal:** When Heavy Bellcopper's kinetic reservoir is fully **EMPTY/Ready**, grant **+100% to final physical Defense Power** (= ×2.00). When Medium Bellcopper's reservoir is fully EMPTY/Ready, grant **+50% to final physical Defense Power** (= ×1.50). Calculate conventional physical Defense Power (effective END² + applicable effective AR², with armor penetration/gap rules) FIRST, then multiply **the final Defense Power linearly just once** as an after-calculation factor; DO NOT double base AR or END before squaring, alter MR, or multiply the attacker's Power. With ANY stored kinetic energy, either class returns to **normal unmodified physical Defense Power**. The bonus is for defending against an actual relevant physical armor-contacting attack when the reservoir is fully empty; its absorption response generates/stores kinetic energy to be released acoustically. This does not create a passive global Defense bonus for unarmored/uncovered sites, psychic attacks or spells producing direct energy without physical impact, nor a free second physical Defense calculation.

**APPROVED THREE-TURN DRAIN PRINCIPLE:** A charged reservoir needs **three turns to empty and reset**; meaningful additional impacts stop it emptying until the wearer's armor has a three-turn break from meaningful hits. Author describes all stored-energy cases as full loss of the special Defense multiplier, not a proportional bonus while partly charged. Acoustic output, when and where sound pulses are released, and interactions with stealth/hearing remain open. Preserve Light Bellcopper's near-total movement quieting and grapple-contact vocal hush; Medium's partial suppression; Heavy's one-hit reservoir specialty. No shield-style durability meter or separate raw-energy accounting is necessary.

**DESIGN QUESTIONS/PROPOSALS NOT YET APPROVED:** The precise trigger defining a 'meaningful hit' and whether an ultimately superficial hit made superficial by the doubled Defense still charges the reservoir. Quill recommends testing 'actual gear-contacting physical strike which would have been at least Solid versus NORMAL Defense Power' to avoid tiny harmless taps resetting the armor indefinitely, but this is NOT yet approved. Quill recommends resolving the charged/ready status upon the eligible intercepted blow and counting recovery at end of the wearer's turn without qualifying additional strikes. Numeric Heavy STR48/inherent SPD−25% versus Boughsteel candidate STR36/−20%, Medium/Light proportionate heavier profiles, are TEST OPTIONS ONLY, not author approvals. The mechanism of spectral/acoustic release, sound AoE/status, physical hit classification, effect of damaged armor and timing of reset remain open. **Examples (not independent approval of any new Power rule):** against END139, QV Full Heavy Bellcopper AR115 gives Defense 19321+13225=32546; empty multiplier x2 yields 65092; a QV 34970.87 attack is Solid normally and Superficial on first interception.


## November 8, 2026 — Oathsilver numbers and oath-assisted attunement APPROVED; Bellcopper three-class sonic identity APPROVED

### Oathsilver latest explicit numeric and narrative approvals

**AUTHOR APPROVES PREVIOUS OATHSILVER PROPOSAL:** Quality I–V Oathsilver provides a **+3% / +6% / +9% / +12% / +15%** bonus, applied **once** to each qualifying *existing installed enchantment's numerical effect*, never a blanket multiplier to total combat Power/Defense or an automatic extra spell rank, action, socket, or status. The approved working **Quality V Full Heavy Oathsilver** defensive baseline is **AR75 / MR60**, before installed enchantment effects. Eligibility and rounding for edge-case nonnumeric effects remain to be defined during enchantment design. Existing crafter-determined enchantment slot count, equipment Soul Strain, and item requirements do not change. Reattunement remains important following changed ownership.

**AUTHOR ADDS APPROVED OATH-BASED ATTUNEMENT DESIGN:** Keeping a sincere personal oath, following through on meaningful commitments and persistent honesty **can speed Oathsilver's attunement**, providing quest/character-building opportunities. Do not hard-code a universal moral alignment system, automatic truth detection, a fixed time discount or a requirement to undertake an arbitrary quest to wear the armor: exact attunement time, credible oath adjudication, sincerity, character-specific vows, transfer rules and shortcuts remain OPEN. This is an affirmative quest design hook and an attunement acceleration principle, not mandatory religious devotion.

### Bellcopper author-approved qualitative armor identity

**APPROVED DISTINCT LIGHT/MEDIUM/HEAVY FUNCTIONS:** Bellcopper already has acoustic resonance properties. **Light Bellcopper** is optimal for stealth: almost silences the wearer's bodily/equipment movement and can **suppress a grappled target's audible cries/vocal output at actual grapple/contact distance** to facilitate stealth takedowns. This is a sound-muffling capability, not an automatic grapple success, free injury or universal prohibition on spellcasting/psychic thought; standard grapple/reach/action rules still matter. **Heavy Bellcopper** is a single-impact kinetic/acoustic defensive specialist, storing/catching kinetic force from a physical strike and **releasing it as sound**. Its main strength is facing **one meaningful physical blow**, not an unlimited barrier against many quick blows. A charged/saturated resonator takes **three turns free from meaningful refreshing physical hits** to fully empty/reset; additional meaningful physical blows **prevent its reservoir from emptying** and consequently keep its best single-blow response unavailable (or sharply reduced). **Medium Bellcopper** combines weaker Light sound muffling (reduces sound but does not eliminate it) and a smaller/weaker version of the Heavy kinetic reservoir. Preserve distinct roles; no extra action, automatic acoustic injury or universally granted MR against sound without further approval.

**MECHANICAL DESIGN STILL OPEN, NOT AUTHOR-APPROVED NUMERICS:** Define exactly how physical strikes enter and leave a reservoir, the first-hit Power/Defense interaction, sound release timing/range/area and statuses, loudness and audible counterplay, what counts as a 'meaningful hit' for reservoir upkeep, whether the wearer chooses audible output direction, the three-turn timing at beginning/end of turn, Light vocal silencing against covered/obstructed targets, MR/AR/STR/SPD and passive Soul Strain. **QUILL TEST PROPOSAL, NOT CANON:** Treat Heavy as starting Ready; on an actual covered physical strike that *would* result in a Solid-or-worse physical injury, once per fully reset reservoir, halve incoming physical Attack Power for the strike and release a distinct sound pulse. Mark the suit Ringing after this one interception. While Ringing, do not halve additional blows; at each of wearer's turn ends without any qualifying meaningful strike, progress a 1/3, 2/3, 3/3 settling count back to Ready, with a qualifying hit resetting settling to zero. Medium may reduce only 25% of incoming Power with the same three-turn settling, rather than becoming a perpetual reduced-hit formula. No free audible attack Power or stun, hearing injury, AoE suppression, automatic perception bypass or silence on allied characters is created by the concept alone. ## November 8, 2026 — Reprise Bronze adaptive armor approved (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**AUTHOR APPROVES THE THREE-CLASS REPRISE BRONZE ARMOR PROPOSAL AND REJECTS AN ENCHANTMENT-SLOT PENALTY:** Reprise Bronze is a shape-memory alloy whose prepared/engraved **armor** can switch seamlessly between **Light (compressed), Medium (balanced) and Heavy (expanded)** configurations **at the start of the wearer's turn, without consuming an action**. The chosen form stays until the next opportunity at the start of the wearer's turn; no switching for each separate attack or reflexive change after seeing an enemy attack. Its **physical weight genuinely decreases on compression** and increases upon expansion, beyond mundane metal rearrangement, but this is reversible item transformation, not a source of freely harvestable material or arbitrary reshaping. This is a deliberate fantasy property compatible with Reprise Bronze's existing prepared-configuration restriction and Smithcraft Memory Form, which does not now grant arbitrary forms. **Armor class changes but coverage extent does not**: Full remains Full, Standard remains Standard, Partial remains Partial. Quality, installed enchantments, associated equipment-derived stat bonuses and Soul Strain do not change just because armor changes form. No special lost enchantment socket or extra enchantment bonus is imposed by the material.

**APPROVED WORKING QUALITY V REPRISE BRONZE ARMOR BENCHMARK (corresponding full-outfit class profiles, compare same coverage):** Light **AR30, MR0, post-quality STR12, inherent SPD−5%**; Medium **AR60, MR0, STR22, SPD−11%**; Heavy **AR100, MR0, STR35, SPD−19%**. Thus working Quality V Full Heavy Boughsteel **AR115**, versus Reprise Bronze's **AR100**, a **15 AR-point** (15% relative to Reprise's AR100) protection difference. Do not describe this as a universal Boughsteel advantage at every quality/class until their other values are approved. Reprise Bronze's MR0 does not negate specific installed magical enhancements or situation-specific defenses; the material itself grants no ordinary general MR. Each selected configuration uses its own actual STR requirement and existing unified equipment-shortfall rules. Existing generic Partial/Standard/Full coverage burden modifiers are still applied as appropriate, without inventing separate coverage-dependent AR or enchanted slot counts.

**STILL OPEN:** Reprise Bronze armor Quality I–IV AR/MR/STR/SPD ladders, how quality and smith preparation affect the transition, non-armor Memory Form options, costs, unusual damage cases and any per-item form customization beyond the standard three armor classes. No new Skill Point requirement or extra MP payment for ordinary transformations was approved. Next individual material to develop is **Oathsilver**, already canonically a long-term attunement and stable-enchantment alloy with reattunement concerns on transfer; do not assume it grants free Soul Strain relief.

## November 8, 2026 — Nullporc AR/MR benchmark and shattering procedure APPROVED

**LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL:** Pure Nullporcelain/Nullporc **physical AR is 0** at every class/quality. The author APPROVES the previously proposed **Quality I–V Full Heavy MR ratings 90 / 105 / 120 / 140 / 170** as **working equipment calibration**, plus **Quality V Light MR85 / Medium MR120 / Heavy MR170**. These are legitimate numeric item reference ratings, not automatically equivalent to physical AR in a yet-unapproved magical Power equation. All covered regions eligible for a Nullporc effect use the appropriate suit MR without material-panel bookkeeping, consistent with existing Partial/Standard/Full coverage and broad-area spell engagement principles. A **separate Quality V Nullporc helmet has MR85 against direct psychic/mental effects including eligible magical fear**, no physical AR and no extra independent stacking atop an intact full-suit applicable MR. Its precise physical support/load in a mixed outfit is left for eventual helmet equipment detail.

**APPROVED QV FULL HEAVY BURDEN BENCHMARK:** Required post-quality support **STR26**, inherent **SPD −15%**; lies between pure Trufula and Boughsteel, satisfying already-approved ordering. Apply existing coverage burden multipliers to this completed QV Full Heavy reference: Partial STR **ceil(26×.6)=16**, SPD **−9%**; Standard STR **ceil(26×.8)=21**, SPD **−12%**; Full STR26, SPD−15%. No new independent hand/boot armor slots or item-level restriction. Other item qualities/classes' exact support/penalty ladders are not numerically finalized by this approval.

**APPROVED NULLPORC SHATTER TRIGGER (on legitimate physical contact with relevant worn armor):** A **Superficial** physical impact changes no armor condition. A **Solid** physical injury result changes **Intact → Cracked** or **Cracked → Shattered**. A **Devastating or Overwhelming** physical result **Shatters immediately**. This reuses actual resolved physical impact severity and needs no shield-like durability meter, no extra item-damage roll, and no automatic second injury to the wearer. Cracked armor retains **100% of applicable MR/other protective function**; Shattered retains **0% until repaired**; wearer healing does not restore gear. Do not shatter a torso suit due solely to a wound from an entirely exposed/unarmored limb; a physical attack must credibly strike the affected Nullporc gear. Energy spells on their own do not crack gear, and a magic-powered physical collision still counts as physical contact for breakage and AR0.

**MAGICAL TEST COMPARATORS (USER-AUTHORIZED FOR LATER TESTING, NOT NEW COMPLETE SORCERY INCANTATIONS):** For convenient eventual comparisons, treat a baseline unaltered **Ignis** fire-energy example and a working-name **Slingshot** magically generated/propelled physical attack as equal to the **Light weapon baseline damage**, each scaling its attacking output from effective **INT** rather than STR. These are illustrative testing profiles only: **Slingshot is not hereby added as an automatically obtainable canon incantation, skill, purchase, or priced alteration**, and Ignis's approved named alterations remain untouched. Detailed spell F, MP, Elemental Magnitude, range, application, and availability are not newly set. Physical Slingshot/rock impact faces ordinary physical AR (Nullporc AR0), not Nullporc MR merely because it is magically launched; direct Ignis energy faces applicable MR and specific energy defenses, subject to later finalized spell resolution.

**APPROVED FUTURE SPELL-CATALOGUE DESIGN PHILOSOPHY:** In general, **direct energy-based spells should deal more damage and provide broader tactical/status options than comparable magic-created physical attacks**. Magic-created or magic-propelled physical attacks remain meaningful deliberate alternatives for counters such as MR-specialist mage hunters and distinct physical attack interactions, **not a roughly equal half of the spell catalogue's damaging choices merely for flavor**. Preserve the wider physical-versus-energy source distinction, material vulnerabilities, psychic mechanics, and existing spell catalogues until specifically designed. No universal damage multiplier or distribution percentage is specified or adopted.

**OPEN FOLLOW-UP:** General MR/Elemental Magnitude/psychic effect formula, Light/Medium MR QI–IV progression, other quality support/Speed numbers, helmet integration and armor repair methods remain pending. No further Sorcery balance test is required *at this stage* per author's preference. Next pure material under review can be Glassweave, if the author wishes to continue individual materials before advanced composites.

**LATEST AUTHOR TERMINOLOGY APPROVAL:** On armor and equipment cards and in future corresponding defense formulas, **AR = Armor Resistance** (the established physical armor-protection number, formerly spelled out **Armor Rating**) and **MR = Magic Resistance** (the protective equipment's applicable magical-defense rating). **AR's NAME changes, not its existing approved numerical values, penetration rules, or physical formula.** Distinguish AR from MR and from the creature's innate/stat-based END, Psychic Resistance and other specific defenses. The new MR shorthand does NOT by itself settle a mathematical treatment of Elemental Magnitude, direct psychic attacks, magical statuses, magic Power, magic bypass, or MR stacking; these remain subject to explicit author approval. Conventional armor generally confers no general-purpose MR by physical AR alone, as approved. Nullporcelain is physically AR0 at all qualities/classes; its MR is exceptionally high in relevant magical contexts. Its partial/full coverage rules, psychic-specific helmet exception, energy-spell non-breakage, indirect physical force bypass, and intact/cracked/full versus Shattered/none remain as previously approved.

**CURRENT NONCANON CALIBRATION PROPOSAL, NOT APPROVED NUMERIC RULE:** Test Full Heavy Nullporc **MR I–V 90/105/120/140/170** by comparison with the approved working Adamantite physical AR Quality I–V numbers, with Quality V Light MR85 and Medium MR120 as initial class candidates, but **do not treat either these MR values or their exact mathematical effects as final canon**. Prior candidate Quality V Full Heavy Nullporc physical support STR26, inherent SPD −15% also remains a proposal within author-approved weight order (Trufula < Nullporc < Boughsteel). Exact shatter triggers and repair remain open. Pure ceramic gear with zero AR should not quietly inherit AR25 joint underpadding.
