## November 8, 2026 — Nullporc intact-until-shattered protection (LATEST AUTHOR CLARIFICATION)

**APPROVED:** **Nullporc** is an accepted shorthand for **Nullporcelain**. Nullporcelain equipment retains **100% of its applicable physical and anti-magic/psychic protective benefits while intact or only lightly damaged/cracked**; there is **no progressive penalty** at the lightly damaged stage. Once an item is **Shattered**, **ALL of that item's protective benefits are negated** until it is actually repaired. Healing its wearer does **not** repair or restore the shattered item. This is item-specific: a shattered helmet loses its psychic protection, and shattered armor loses its own physical and magical protection; do not arbitrarily erase benefits of separate intact equipment. Physical injury-to-gear breakage trigger, thresholds, repair procedure and numerical AR/spell protection remain to be proposed and approved. Do **not** silently apply shield durability counters or finalize a crack/shatter trigger from an earlier assistant proposal. Existing coverage and actual hit/contact requirements remain in effect.


## November 8, 2026 — Armor vs Elemental Magnitude philosophy ratified; Nullporcelain design brief

**AUTHOR-RATIFIED DESIGN PHILOSOPHY:** Conventional armor's ordinary physical Armor Rating does **not** itself reduce Elemental Magnitude and does **not** protect against direct psychic/mind effects. Elemental and supernatural defenses depend on legitimate protective materials, enchantments, insulation, conductivity, barriers, obstruction and applicable specific Resistance. Actual physical projectiles/impacts generated by spells are resolved as physical threats (with relevant physical armor), not granted Nullporcelain anti-spell benefit merely because magic propelled them. Do not negate END, special Resistances, meaningful pathways, or established Sorcery mechanics. Exact Elemental Magnitude and material-specific reduction calculations remain open.

**NULLPORCELAIN AUTHOR DESIGN DIRECTION, PRE-NUMERIC (not final stat rules):** Nullporcelain is deliberately fragile against physical blows, offers low physical protection and may genuinely break; favor a minimal breakage procedure linked to qualifying actual physical injury rather than separate shield-style HP/durability counters. Armor degradation persists after its wearer is magically or mundanely healed and requires actual equipment repair. Ordinary energy-spell exposure does **not** itself crack Nullporcelain. It is a premium mage-hunting material that greatly reduces intensity/effect of incoming directly magical spells; anti-magic defense must not also block indirectly generated physical impacts such as rocks launched by magic. Conventional attack/armor coverage is preserved for targeted attacks. Ordinary broad magical area attacks should generally assess a worn Nullporcelain outfit's magical protection as hit/engaged directly, subject to actual delivery and genuinely unprotected-path exceptions; this does not make exposed skin physically armored or grant blanket protection from separate physical debris. A **distinct Nullporcelain HELMET** is desired as a rare, deliberate exception to all-of-one-outfit material uniformity: anti-psychic and mental-spell protection, including fear spells, without forcing per-boot, per-arm, or per-panel bookkeeping. The helmet's protection, interaction with area effects, cost/load, and physical targeting must be bounded when numerical mechanics are proposed. No breakage-trigger bands, AR, magnitude reductions, helmet benefits or new SP/MP fees have yet been approved.

**QUILL'S UNAPPROVED SIMPLIFICATION TO TEST:** On an actual physically armor-contacting injurious attack, use the physical injury severity to mark Nullporcelain Cracked or Shattered; superficial contact and direct psychic wounds do not crack it; a broken state persists despite wearer healing. Do not automatically crack torso armor because the wearer was wounded through an uncovered hand. This is a proposal and needs author approval alongside statistics.

**LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL:** In the already-approved Quality V Full Heavy reference composite, **Trufula/Boughsteel AR increases from 105 to 110** (superseding the prior 105 reference), while its full-coverage post-quality **support STR28, inherent SPD −16%** and universal coverage multipliers stay fixed. Trufula/Adamantite is unchanged: **AR135 / STR55 / −21%** in Full, with prior Partial/Standard burden values. These remain named benchmark constructions, not a recipe to automatically generate every mixture.

**AUTHOR'S PRESENTATION/PHILOSOPHY DECISION:** Composite equipment is worth retaining even if some pairings have narrower competitive roles than others. At publication/delivery, keep composite engineering and calculations on its **own reference page**, separate from the clear, approachable primary materials overview, so ordinary equipment distinctions are easy to understand before optional optimization. Quality V armor is expected to reflect extensive expensive crafting mastery, rare resources, and unusual wealth; strong protection at early character levels is not inherently a reason to nerf it. No new acquisition-level gate is imposed.

**ARMOR AND NONPHYSICAL ATTACKS:** Author highlights that **conventional physical armor does not protect against direct psychic attacks**. Preserve physical Armor Rating's scope: a truly direct psychic/mind attack does not add ordinary armor AR² to defense; effective END and relevant Psychic Resistance or technique-specific defenses apply. Elemental Magnitude remains unfinalized numerically: ordinary armor does NOT automatically supply generic Elemental Magnitude reduction simply from its physical AR, but actual insulating, conducting, shielding or blocking construction may alter the delivered elemental effect as relevant under existing material/contact rules. This is the recommended interpretation of existing elemental principles, **not** an author approval of a universal new numeric element-mitigation formula. Exact elemental mitigation calculations/thresholds remain OPEN; the author has asked for the rationale, not yet explicitly ratified new universal rules.


## November 8, 2026 — Universal armor coverage burden and uniform composite protection (LATEST AUTHOR DIRECTION)

**AUTHOR DIRECTION:** Minimize complexity; Partial / Standard / Full coverage use ONE universal physical-burden schedule for all armor classes and materials, and a composite's stated main-surface AR is uniform across its actually covered main surfaces. Do not separately calculate breastplate, arm, boot, or material-panel AR within one specified composite profile. True uncovered targets remain AR0; Medium/Heavy protected structural joints retain their established built-in AR25; actual armor class and coverage must still determine what is covered. No separate glove/boot weight ledger.

**FIXED COVERAGE BURDEN VALUES:** Relative to the Full-coverage armor of the *same class, material composition and quality*, Partial armor takes **60%** of Full support STR and **60%** of Full inherent SPD-penalty magnitude; Standard **80%** of both; Full **100%** of both. Round resulting STR UP to a whole point, and round resulting inherent SPD penalty to the nearest whole percentage point, halves upward. Do not reduce AR on legitimately covered main surfaces; the same wearer's effective SPD is multiplied once by `(1 - final inherent penalty / 100)`, plus separately applicable established penalties. Quality's approved physical requirement discount applies BEFORE coverage scaling; do not apply quality twice. These are armor *coverage* rules, not a new material AR conversion equation, enchantment bonus, or shield rating rule.

**FINAL QV FULL HEAVY COMPOSITE REFERENCE PROFILES, as previously author-approved:** substantial Trufula/Boughsteel **AR105 / support STR28 / inherent SPD −16%**; substantial Trufula/Adamantite **AR135 / STR55 / SPD −21%**. Their derived coverages: Trufula/Boughsteel **Partial STR17, −10%; Standard STR23, −13%; Full STR28, −16%**. Trufula/Adamantite **Partial STR33, −13%; Standard STR44, −17%; Full STR55, −21%**. These two are reference engineered constructions, not an unlimited material mixing recipe; no extra independent layering or best-of-all-material bonuses. The earlier pure Full Heavy material-quality AR ladders and composite Fire ×2 / valid conductive Lightning ×3 weaknesses remain unchanged. Specific pure-material support/Speed candidates, other armor-class base requirements, universal mixing equations, costs, ignition thresholds and precise elemental source/Resistance ordering remain separate unfinished design tasks, not silently finalized here.
# Mystaria — Canon Changelog, Supersession Ledger, and Open Questions

## November 8, 2026 — Guard Flow final design selected: Perfect Pivot with Master's Answer (LATEST)

- **Approved design choice:** Guard Flow's final stance upgrade is **Perfect Pivot (option A)** combined with the *specific learned tactical response* aspect of **Master's Answer (option F)**. It is a stance affordance following meaningful successful defense, not a Fangscript technique, not combo-spending, not a simple wound/stat bonus, and not automatic attack generation. It supersedes the former *Perfect Reversal* and the previous *capstone undecided* status. **Exact progression/rank, cost, action-economy timing, exchange-frequency, thresholds and prediction persistence remain pending.**
- **Guard progression direction:** Read the Blade to learn genuine tendencies; Break the Pattern to overcome physical tactical disadvantages; Perfect Pivot to transform a qualified defense into positional/engagement advantage, exploiting actual enemy commitment and observed tactics.
- **Approved anti-magic identity:** Kharak counter spells through better **physical evasion, positioning, viable cover, timing of actual delivery interruption, and observation of repeating casting habits**, *not* extra magic Resistance, blanket spell cancellation, reflected spells, or reduced elemental damage by default. **Proposed examples only:** Blastline Pivot, Broken Cadence, Spell's Blindside, Master's Answer. AoE requires actual escape beyond its footprint; silent/instant/counter-contingent spells are not automatically vulnerable to interruption. Casting interruption specifics remain a general mechanical open item. No change to Sorcery's Wyrmcraft, action economy, Shimmer Guard, Pressure or elemental calculations.
- **Updated files:** `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Guard Flow tactical direction and Fangscript meditation approved (LATEST)

- **APPROVED:** Kharak natural red elemental Warflow effects manifest fire; compatible **Drakyn elemental dragonmarks may alter those manifestations**. Detailed cross-craft mark interface, precise eligible marks, costs, Soul Strain and alternate-element mechanics are **OPEN**, and existing dragonmarks do not universally rewrite unrelated Martial Arts or grant missing mana colors.
- **APPROVED Guard Flow stage-2 development objective:** meaningful high-combo defenses actively defeat specific enemy advantages from flanking, offhand/dual-wield threats, ranged suppression and long-reach weapon control, possibly break up the close **Concentrated Burst ×1.35 final Power** situation by legitimate evasion/redirection. No automatic universal anti-burst immunity, no auto-Pressure subtraction, no grant of free attacks or override of shield directional limits. Numeric thresholds/rank mechanics remain unapproved.
- **GUARD STAGE 3 OPEN:** *Perfect Reversal* rejected as redundant with Guard Fangscript and must be replaced. Prefer meaningful non-combo-spending tactical option, not mirrored cheap attacks or simple resistance/wound inflation. Six capstone candidates are **PROPOSALS**: Perfect Pivot, Unwritten Move, Hold the Line, Thousand-Fang Awareness, Stolen Initiative, Master's Answer. Older inverted-wound proposal reserved **as possible Fangscript technique only**, not approved.
- **AUTHOR-APPROVED meditation procedure** (upgrades earlier numerical proposal): every uninterrupted **6 seconds** outside hostile exchanges yields `1 + floor(effective INT / 20)` prepared momentum; **10-minute lifespan**, consumption on Fangscript use, current relevant combo cap, no automatic Warflow stance passive buffs, and no doubling resource capacity through a separate full prepared pool. No meditation farming during combat, no extra attacks. Which Attack/Guard pool applies and specific Fangscript costs still pending. Starting effective Kharak INT 21.25 earns 2 per six seconds; 3-cap fills in 12 seconds.
- **Updated:** `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`. No older Drakyn/Sorcery, Pressure Point, recoil, shield or reach-control rules rewritten.


## November 8, 2026 — Warflow split into separate skills and single-stance limit (LATEST DIRECTION)

- **Author clarification:** Attack Flow and Guard Flow are **two distinct skills** with separate progression, not merely two modes of a single skill. A martial artist receives **only one stance's benefits at any moment**. Intended combo maximum grows from **3 untrained** to **8 fully mastered**; rank-by-rank assignment, costs and precise bonuses remain for approval.
- **Attack Flow preferred stance progression:** combo-scaled elemental attack imbuement, elemental movement at higher combo, and final small flat Fangscript Attack Combo discount. Any fire/ice elemental crossover must obey later approved affinity rules.
- **Guard Flow explicitly under redesign:** user is skeptical of a passive DEX/ignored-wounds/reversed-wounds package and prefers **new tactical options at high combo**, not merely increased survival numbers. Enemy-reading, redirection, ally protection and conditional counters suggested only, not approved.
- **User's PROPOSED meditation values:** ~6 seconds per preparation interval, +1 momentum per 20 effective INT, ~10 minutes duration, spent on Fangscript. Formula `1 + floor(INT / 20)`, whether prepared momentum activates stances, cap-sharing, action interruptions and final duration remain **open proposals**. Prior direct red-MP Fangscript option remains intended, inefficient, unpriced. This is a design note, not SP/MP numerical canon.
- **Files updated:** `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Thornguard reserve and Kharak Arts design direction (LATEST)

- **Approved Thornguard change:** natural white MP is **2 × floor(effective END)** (level-1 END 20 = 40 MP). They retain no direct self-casting/shaping/spending. **Equipment Soul Strain incurs ordinary full strain** but has a **hard ceiling of 50% of doubled natural MP** (20 in example), preserving other half of *capacity* for cooperative use, **not guaranteeing unspendable current MP**. Other Soul Strain origins remain governed by their own rules; any global total-cap interaction remains unresolved.
- **Approved access principle:** Thornguard and ordinary Moontouched may qualify for genuinely passive/mana-free or otherwise lawfully externally powered Crafts, alchemy/crafting and Jestercraft where the owner prerequisites permit; neither gains unauthorized active personal mana shaping or ordinary Sorcery. Chosen-Warlock exceptions preserved. Mana transfers to allies/wells require compatible established interfaces, not automatic free self-release.
- **Author's Kharak Martial Art development direction:** Warflow and Fangscript work together. Warflow has distinct Attack and Guard combo-building development, sustained red mana investment can enhance benefits and combo ceiling; Fangscript uses named stat-scaling physical supernatural techniques with significant noncombat use via brief meditation/preparation and very inefficient red-MP direct invocation without combo. This direction is to be developed; **no numeric mechanics, SP prices, full rank skills, definitive named moves, or power scaling are approved**. Attack/Guard combo is separate from existing Pressure Points.
- **Author's Thornguard Arts direction:** mana-free, highly enduring, somewhat lower scaling and rich combat decisions; details still to be designed. ## November 7, 2026 — Kharak profane lexicon approved (LATEST)

- **Approved:** “Fangrot!” as signature everyday curse; “Arrest's Arse!” as profane invocation; “Rot your word!” as the corrected curse; and commonplace “Coward!”.
- **Approved profane lines:** “Fuck your banner.”, “You couldn't lead shit downhill.”, “Piss-for-brains.”, “Shitjaw.”, “Arse-biter.”, “You've got more balls than brains.”, “Go chew rocks.”, and “I'll shove that title up your arse.” Kharak speech is often rough and vulgar, with especially gentle family speech toward hatchlings.

- Existing approved “Count them home.” and “My word stands.” / “Then I stand with it.” remain in force. Other proposed language/fashion details remain unapproved.

## November 7, 2026 — Kharak signature phrases approved; vulgar lexicon pending (LATEST)

- **Approved:** “Count them home.” as a Kharak safety/return farewell; “My word stands.” and “Then I stand with it.” as a customary, nonmagical commitment and response.
- **Author correction:** use **“Rot your word.”** in place of proposed “Rot your oath.”
- **Design direction:** Kharak profanity should be genuinely vulgar, rough and inventive, comparable in colorful impact to Tidekin swearing. “Cracked shell!” is rejected as too egg-centered for the everyday staple; “Broken fang!” and alternatives are not yet approved. Newly proposed curses and insults remain proposals.

## November 7, 2026 — Vornak history intentionally left open; Kharak production order (LATEST)

- **Author direction:** preserve Vornak's detailed biography, motives, campaigns and chronology as deliberately **open** for future plotlines; do not invent finalized history beyond previously approved broad canon.
- **Development order:** Kharak speech and linguistic customs first; then casual/uniform clothing; then collars coordinated with Tattao; finally three-quarter-view master racial artwork. New designs and idioms remain proposals until expressly approved.

## November 7, 2026 — Hatchbane evidence and Deep Inquiry procedure approved (LATEST)

- **Approved:** Court of Fangs addresses motive, opportunity and means; authenticated **mirror evidence** and eyewitness accounts are favored, with independent corroboration and consideration of exculpatory evidence. Free-folk testimony traditionally receives greater weight than enslaved testimony, but corroborated enslaved testimony is admissible. Direct proof can establish guilt without separately proving a specific motive.
- **Approved thresholds:** Third Degree **preponderance of evidence**, Second Degree **clear and convincing**, First Degree **beyond reasonable doubt**. First Degree normally calls for two independent evidentiary foundations; independently authenticated exceptionally clear mirror evidence can suffice.
- **Approved five stages:** petition/protection, investigation, hearing, judgment, sentence/review. Include Third-Degree welfare follow-up, Banebrand identity verification and independent review before irreversible punishment.
- **Approved Writ of Deep Inquiry:** narrowly scoped, independent Crown Arbiter authorization of dangerous compelled deep mind reading or memory extraction only for exceptionally grave unresolved cases, with substantial independent evidence, exhaustion of safer methods, necessity/proportionality, scope limits, witnessed documentation and restrictions on unrelated discoveries. Informed consent governs voluntary limited examination. Free persons' independent rights to silence and mental privacy remain recognized; invoking them is not proof of guilt.
- Outstanding specifics include examiner qualifications, appeals timing, slave protections, and writ refusal/remedies. Supersedes earlier entries marking these procedures as proposals.

## November 7, 2026 — Mirror evidence and separate mental privacy/silence rights (LATEST)

- **Approved correction:** mirrors record visual/audio information; Orbs **store** mirror recordings but cannot directly record sight or sound. Call video/audio recordings **mirror evidence**, not 'Orb recordings'. Authenticated mirror evidence and eyewitness accounts are favored; Imperial courts tend to favor free-folk over enslaved eyewitness testimony.
- **Approved:** psionic mind reading, Orbcraft memory extraction and Tidekin Mirrorwake may support innocence or guilt, but are not infallible. Compelled deep mind reading or memory transfer is painful, dangerous, potentially permanently harmful, and restricted in principle to very high-stakes cases with extensive independent evidence when ordinary proof remains inconclusive. Trivial emotion-reading capability does not imply unrestricted admissibility.
- **Approved:** free people hold separate rights to **silence** and **mental privacy**. They can invoke each independently; unconsented emotional readings cannot be used as evidence or grounds for suspicion. Masters have significant authority over whether and how their slaves are questioned; exact bounds remain open.
- **PROPOSED ONLY:** a special **Writ of Deep Inquiry**, requiring independent Crown Arbiter authorization, necessity, proportionality, exhaustion of safer means and a limited scope. Prior suggested evidence thresholds and court workflow are also pending explicit approval.

## November 7, 2026 — Kharak compassion toward other races' children (LATEST)

- **Approved:** Kharak regard acts comparable to Hatchbane committed against children of **other races** as especially disgusting, though the protection of their own young remains more intense. This is primarily **informal cultural condemnation**, not an automatic expansion of the formal Kharak Hatchbane offense. It provides grounds for cross-cultural kinship, adopted/protected outsiders, and soldiers' crises of conscience.
- **Approved historical direction:** generations of Kharak families, elders and warriors began rejecting ancestral Extinction practices before Vornak; he helped give the emerging protective ethic the force of law rather than single-handedly inventing it. Exact legislative history remains open.
- **Still PROPOSED:** three-part motive/opportunity/means review, evidentiary preference for authenticated Orb records and free-folk eyewitnesses, tiered proof thresholds, and court sentencing workflow. No new procedural specifics canonized pending author approval.

## November 7, 2026 — Hatchbane degrees, ancient substitution and Kharak Extinction past (LATEST)

- **Approved Third Degree:** substantial neglect/disrespect, poor food/lodging and abusive care, including failures caused by compulsory slave duties; reparations, often humiliating apology, mandatory excellence in subsequent care; repeated failure may escalate.
- **Approved Second Degree:** substantial harm or kidnapping of eggs/hatchlings, including attack upon a Kharak carrying an unlaid egg; court establishes seriousness and culpability. Attacker and superior who culpably allowed it may share guilt, not automatic strict liability. **Banebrand** is a permanently painful, nonhealing magical mark, with possible clan reorganization. Repeat Hatchbane after Banebrand may bring loss of station or death; higher rank implies greater responsibility, not immunity.
- **Approved First Degree:** deliberate killing, attempted killing, exceptionally grave abuse, commissioned assassination of eggs/hatchlings, and foreseeable/accepted killing during commissioned kidnapping. Command responsibility is evaluated through proof of culpability. Loss of title and Coil; **Decimation** by a follower of Arrest for those without comparable status, subject to further accountability for powerful offenders. Exact procedure open.
- **Ancient exception:** almost never invoked in independent, non-Arrest Kharak clans, not ordinary Imperial law: a freely volunteering **independent adult offspring** may offer life as substitute. Only a **true Serpentari** has ancestral authority to rescind the old provision. Coercion invalidates the offer; enormous consequences and continuing dishonor. Its historical origin and formal operation remain unresolved.
- **Historical contrast:** early Khans before Vornak practiced **Extinction**, destroying enemy bloodlines and sometimes entire minor species, including children. Modern Hatchbane reflects Kharak movement toward protective justice, albeit imperfectly. Vornak's precise reforms remain open. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## November 7, 2026 — True Serpentari four-name semantics and inherited-to-earned Ascendancy approved (LATEST)

- The author approves the four ancient true Serpentari name positions: **Self** (parent-given personal identity), **Blood** (ancestral line), **Great Coil** (aristocratic dynasty and political belonging), **Ascendancy** (a name inherited at birth that can later be **replaced by a name earned** through personal achievement). An inherited Ascendancy need not be replaced and is not automatically dishonorable. Additional ceremonial, conquest and regnal titles remain possible. Existing two-name Untouched Serpentari conventions remain distinct.
- The established Great Coils, Coilkeepers, Nine Thrones and First Sovereign remain operative. **Vaessarith Theryn Voryss Xalquorin** is a naming example, not yet the approved identity of Vornak's opposing monarch.
- Earlier proposals concerning Hatchbane's culpability distinctions, investigation of missing eggs, and a special emergency parental petition to the Court of Fangs are approved as **design direction**: intentional offenses and accidents should not be conflated; an accusation alone is not conviction; parents can seek urgent intervention, without gaining unrestricted authority to mutiny. Exact elements, petition standing, safeguards, and penalties remain unresolved.


## November 7, 2026 — Terminology correction (LATEST)

- **Hatchbane** is the approved Kharak crime name; **hatch-traitor** remains the perpetrator. This corrects the previous mistaken spelling *Harchband*, which is superseded and not canon.

## November 7, 2026 — Hatchbane approved as Kharak hatchling crime (LATEST)

- **Hatchbane** is the author-approved name of the particularly heinous crime of harming a Kharak egg or hatchling. Its perpetrators are called **hatch-traitors** (singular **hatch-traitor**).
- This **supersedes** the earlier unapproved suggestion *Hatchtreason* / *Hatchtraitor*. Exact legal elements, levels of culpability, penalties, missing-egg investigations, and emergency challenge procedure remain undecided. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## November 7, 2026 — True Serpentari four proper names and Kharak hatchling-crime terminology request (LATEST)

- **Approved naming form:** Ancient true Serpentari have **four proper names**, optionally followed by additional titles. Combine aristocratic dynastic tradition (previous option A) with elevated conquest/achievement regnal titulature (option C), making the names more alien/exotic. Preserve distinct approved two-name **Untouched Serpentari** convention unless later expressly revised.
- Existing **Great Coils**, **Coilkeepers**, **Nine Thrones**, and chosen **First Sovereign** remain controlling. A Serpentari historical 'High King' must be reconciled with First Sovereign rather than presumed an unrelated monarchy.
- **PROPOSAL, not canon:** Four slots as Self, Blood (lineage), Great Coil (dynasty), Ascendancy (earned/provisional adult identity). Example Vaessarith Theryn Voryss Xalquorin. Formal semantics await author confirmation.
- **PROPOSAL, not canon:** **Hatchtreason** as Kharak offense covering knowing harm, abduction or culpable endangerment of eggs/hatchlings, and **Hatchtraitor** as condemnation of culprit. Alternatives Broodslaughter and Hearthbane. Differentiating deliberate violence, negligence, and accidents is important; no criminal statute or mandatory sentence is yet approved.


## November 7, 2026 — Kharak Hearths, Warclans, Tattao, Arrest worship and Vornak approved (LATEST)

- **Family:** Hearth homes, extended multihousehold Warclans and wider Imperial Coil allegiance; adoption, shared hatchling care, martial education, affection, and clanlessness as severe social burden. Both mothers and fathers may lead by merit.
- **Hatchlings:** Intensive protective ethos; harm to a hatchery can provoke furious collective challenges even where ordinary challenge law grants direct standing to the First Fang. Special emergency procedures and collective standing are **UNRESOLVED**, not automatically granted.
- **Legal exception:** **Kharak offspring are exempt from hereditary slavery**, overriding the Gilded Coil's general maternal-status rule for this race. The **First Oath** recognizes teenage warrior responsibilities to Warclan; the **Second Oath** at army-service age is the culturally acclaimed voluntary entry into Coil service, ordinarily accompanying their **Tattao**. Exact ages and legal safeguards remain undefined; rare Kharak refuse or depart.
- **Tattao:** Unique named Kharak allegiance tattoos aligned visually with collars, prominently displaying their Coil and secondarily Warclan. These are **belonging marks**, distinct from Corvyn witnessing/Ink Name art. Transferring allegiance is consequential; exact designs and retattooing procedures open.
- **Faith:** Imperial Kharak chiefly honor **Arrest** with an interpretation emphasizing fair challenge and familial responsibility; independent wild clans often follow **Baphomet**.
- **Names and Vornak:** Approved personal + Warclan + earned title pattern; **Kharzug Vornak, Khan of the Unbroken Word**, commonly called **Vornak** when quoted. His descendants remain in the highest-ranked Matriarch's service as the most prestigious Kharak lineage. Examples Kharzug, Thorgat, Borghan, Durgan, Varkul, Ghorak, Vraksha, Kharza, Rhazka, Tharka, Zhurra, Vorga; Warclans Vornak, Kharvul, Thrazgor, Gorvash and Dravok.
- **Historic ruling:** The **Honored Defeat** is chosen. Vornak accepted an authentic strategic defeat by an ancient Serpentari monarch; the sovereign technically honored the compact but his empire enslaved and heavily restrained the Kharak. Historic contest is ancestral in spirit to modern Golden Conquest; modern sport rules are unchanged. Serpentari king's personal name and exact chronology not yet approved.
- Owning file: `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`. Detailed Tattao patterns, literal ceremony scripting, legal transfer process, numeric stats and High King name remain **OPEN**.


## November 7, 2026 — Kharak supplemental approvals: cultural exchange, Court of Fangs, Khan's maxims (LATEST)

- Author approved earlier suggestions: Kharak–Naga mutual cultural influence in military frankness, tactical prestige, recorded rank, stable Coil duty, law and precedent, blended Imperial luxury/military adornment, and cross-cultural speech.
- **Court of Fangs**, **Oathspeaker** advocates and **Crown Arbiter** judges are approved names. Drakyn are especially favored for major arbitration; routine cases do not always require one. Evidence must establish material failure rather than rumor. Transfer of warrior service does not automatically transfer estates/debts. Precise procedures and jurisdiction remain open.
- **Khan of the Unbroken Word** is the approved epithet for the historic great Khan. Three approved sayings: “A blade wins a battle. A command wins a war. A law preserves the victory.”; “Challenge your superior before his mistakes become your soldiers' graves.”; “The strongest warrior is not the one who never kneels. It is the one who knows whom to kneel before.” The link to an ancestral strategic contest leading toward modern **Golden Conquest** is approved in direction, without retroactively imposing modern Orb mechanics.
- **Base INT 17** is an approved *design target*, not a completed racial stat sheet; 15 is the minimum desired if constrained. Actual stat configuration is unresolved.
- **Still unapproved:** The Khan's personal and clan names, family and naming customs, final contest outcome (honored vs betrayed), opponent High King's identity, legal fine print, fashion and numeric mechanics. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.


## November 7, 2026 — Kharak name, Rexfolk philosophy, military hierarchy and challenges approved (LATEST)

- **Name approved:** **Kharak**, with Rexfolk a descriptive everyday designation. The new reference is `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.
- **Race:** Very large tyrannosaur mortal warrior people; common but fewer than Duskpaws, main feared heavy combatants of the Naga Imperium; ordinary work-slave employment rare. Desired red affinity, greatest playable-race Strength, huge bite and oversized weapons; precise mechanics **not yet established**.
- **Intelligence:** Author specifically rejects the dumb-brute archetype; wants base INT minimum **15**, prefers **17** if stat balancing permits. These numbers are design constraints, **not an approved final race sheet**.
- **Approved worldview:** Belonging to a worthy hierarchy is honorable; unwanted freedom and clanlessness can be humiliating. Allegiance is tied to earned competence, not meek surrender; independent clans may act as mercenaries or bandits and violently resist unearned captivity. A defeated clan may elect to swear service.
- **Approved ranks:** Fang, Bloodfang, Clawleader, Warhowler, Warclaw, Warchief, High Warchief. **First Fang** is the senior Rexfolk servant immediately beneath a Matriarch, distinct from a universal military rank.
- **Origins:** Powerful unstable nomadic/clan conquerors, disunited by betrayal; ancient Serpentari ruined their homeland and kept them under severe physical/magical leashes as living weapons. A later Rexfolk ideological tradition paradoxically praises the imposed order; Naga then fostered closer military and cultural partnerships. Exact mutual fashions/customs not yet approved.
- **Right of Challenge:** First Fang has standing and responsibility to challenge evidence-backed leadership failures, including failed stewardship of small household gangs, major campaigns and economic/reputational collapse. Documentary precedent and clear terms matter. Legitimate reassignment inside the Imperial hierarchy is distinct from desertion/rebellion; overzealous challengers may be punished or demoted. Competing Matriarch offers or redeployment of modest units are recognized possibilities. **Drakyn** favored as legal intermediaries/judges; court titles and detailed procedure are still proposals.
- **Founding Khan:** Author established the intended concept of a great near-unifier and intellectual/martial lawgiver, eventually defeated by a Serpentari in a nonviolent test of power associated with the origin of Imperial sports. Identity, nature of that contest and historical particulars remain **OPEN**, and must respect approved `17_Imperial_Sports_and_Great_Coil_Circuit.md`.

Exceptionally common torn, mismatched, repaired, reused and mixed attire, Cache sharing and heirloom history, patchwork formal outfits, and reclaimed open-lock ornaments are cultural patterns.
- **Lastwear:** Retain definition as clothing **visibly associated with its former wearer's death**. Salvaged, memorial and trophy Lastwear are **descriptive motives**, not official grades. Real signs can be repaired but remain noticeable; intentional fake battle damage is ordinarily mocked as pretentious waste, though responses vary by person and grief context.

Owners often demand serviceable and regulated work uniforms, even if a Duskpaw prefers haphazard salvage dress. This does not authorize any particular proposed uniform pattern yet.

## November 6, 2026 — Bloomkin Bannerweave, hero introductions and First Fitting approved (LATEST)

- **Explicit author approval:** **Bannerweave** names the Bloomkin-led shared fashion tradition of Thornguard knights and Bloomkin Shieldmates, using **complementary heraldry** rather than identical miniature outfits. **Twinmantle** denotes particularly synchronized paired presentation. Bloomkin are often their knights' personal tailors; the first coordinated joint wardrobe is the important artistic milestone **First Fitting**.
- **Aesthetic and personality:** Bloomkin seek to **inspire awe** through theatrical, expertly sewn hero-and-squire attire and revere heroes of the gods and legends. Their romanticized notions of a hero may be naive, but their craft is exceptionally skilled. **Purple** is held in particular reverence; no compulsory palette or added magic.
- **Speech and social custom:** Fanciful, exuberant self-introductions and self-proclaimed champion titles are common among Bloomkin squires, Shieldmates and celebrated craftspeople, e.g. “the greatest wielder of the white” or “the Hope of the Canopy.” Thornguard partners often pretend disapproval while being endeared. These are expressive epithets, **not** changes to personal/craft family names, registered titles or true achievements.
- **Reputation and bonds:** Legendary Thornguard may attract many skilled Bloomkin eager to be their Shieldmate. Tailors and magic shapers should generally be treated with care because of their importance, without abolishing the historic **Oath of Protection**, legislating benevolence, or altering Concordance. Thornguard still supply white; Bloomkin shape it. 
- **Controlling document:** `31_Bloomkin_Bannerweave_Fashion.md`; cross-reference `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, `02_Races.md`, `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`, `24_Racial_Naming_Conventions_and_Namebook.md`, `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md`.
- **Limits:** No new SP costs, armor, magic, mandated uniform, legal heraldic rank or fixed selection ceremony. Existing Bloomkin Maid's Choker/Petalwork visual distinctions are preserved.

## November 6, 2026 — Tidekin fashion and everyday swimwear approved (LATEST)

- **Explicit author approval:** Approved the full proposed Tidekin Imperial clothing direction (aquatic practicality, Kin-knot adornment, Saltbound workwear, colorful free Tidekin expression, and movement-oriented formal dress), with **close-fitting swimwear** preferred and **bikini-style tops very common in casual clothing**.

## November 6, 2026 — Kobold Presentation and Demonstration Dress approved (LATEST)

- **Explicit author approval:** All six sections of the Kobold **Presentation Dress versus Demonstration Dress** proposal, including the cherished **Working Coat**, the practical-versus-symbolic use of the **Proud Tool**, and their implications for free and enslaved professionals. The distinction is a widespread **cultural convention**, not mandated clothing or a statutory costume category.
- **Presentation Dress:** Clean and composed professor-style attire for lectures, court, examinations and formal audiences; may include academic coats, fitted waistcoats, short skirts/petticoats, spectacles, polished instruments and appropriate Orb interfaces. It is not restricted to rich Kobolds.
- **Demonstration Dress:** Functional worn, repaired or scorched workwear appropriate for live invention demonstrations, practical examinations, competitions and laboratory work. Genuine work wear may be respected, but artificially inflicted damage does not signify expertise; safety and useful tools still matter.
- **Working Coat:** Familiar patches, sleeves and custom pockets can give an engineer's older coat personal and technical value beyond its price; even a wealthy free inventor may favor one.
- **Proud Tool:** Important tools or personal inventions may be displayed ceremonially in Presentation Dress, or used as actual working implements in Demonstration Dress. Decorative patron gifts such as golden replicas are possible, **not required** or proof of skill.
- **Class and autonomy:** Owner-maintained uniforms and Naga expectations may clash with free Kobold informality. The difference is rooted in clothing choice and control, not innate competence or a foolproof legal-status indicator.
- **Owning file:** `27_Kobold_Imperial_Fashion.md`; index, visual guide and Imperial Gazetteer cross-referenced. **Still PROPOSAL:** formalized Academic Gala festivals, rigid tool-wearing etiquette, official patch rankings and prescribed professor uniforms. No numeric mechanics, status rules or eyesight changes.


## November 6, 2026 — Kobold fashion, eyesight, and professional independence (LATEST)

- **Approved author clarification:** Kobold dress remains practical even for prestigious household specialists: mechanic-like and professor-like garments, notably petticoats. Tailored coats and scholarly accessories are compatible design examples, not a mandatory uniform. Established utility belts, goggles, fitted lab coats, Orb interfaces and restrained leather/latex remain valid.
- **Racial biology:** Kobold eyes generally have weak long-distance vision as cave adaptations; spectacles are notably common in casual dress. Kobolds imprinted onto sharp-sighted races such as Corvyn can be exceptions. **UNRESOLVED:** visual ranges, heredity/physiological mechanism of imprint exceptions, any stat or combat effect. No new numerical racial penalties or imprint-derived skill grants.

Enslaved high-skill household specialists may wear neat, maintained technical/academic clothing. These are tendencies, not absolute legal-status tests.

Formalized gala institutions, mandated outfits, strict tool etiquette and patch rankings remain proposals.

## November 6, 2026 — Naga fashion, matriarch circlets and ceremonial chain symbolism (LATEST)

- **Explicit author approval:** Complete *The Silks and Chains of the Eternal Coil* Naga fashion dossier approved with final author clarifications. Imperial sensual attire emphasizes short dancer-like silks, frequently bare shoulders/midriffs, and elaborate jewelry, rather than excessive fabric. Social status is shown through composition and ornament, not skin coverage.

Light or heavy, often lavishly jeweled pieces are deliberately impractical emblems of standing and freedom. Actual self-binding is **exceptional**, chiefly by zealots or abolitionists deliberately making a statement, not a standard rite. Remembrance, succession, ancestral household observances and conservative court practices are cultural traditions with local variation, not mandatory magic or procedures.

## November 6, 2026 — Three slavery philosophies, regional collars, Orbcraft and magical restraints (LATEST)

**Explicit author approvals:**
- Naga enslave for **order and hierarchy**; Thornguard enslave to **protect the weak whether they like it or not**; Vren enslave to **exploit the weak and grow their power shamelessly**. Place this comparison at the first world-level explanation of slavery.
- **Duskpaw open locks** signify emancipation. The regional collar direction is approved: Imperial polish/registration, Scarlands repaired personalized wear, Canopy heraldic/botanical craft. Imperial visual classification is mixed public/official, with luxury depending on class and gags/muzzles used for both ceremonial/disciplinary and security reasons, while ordinary work remains unsilenced.
- **Kobold Orbs** directly process Cipherlight; Magic Mirrors convert images into/from Cipherlight. Both can control Kobold machinery. Orbcraft practitioners can directly understand/manipulate the information stream. The functional Orb Collar has one central colored Orb, smooth metal, no exposed restraint hardware; a paw-operated local aurora gives unskilled observers a mild headache, but communicates rich information to Orbcrafters. Extreme Rainbows can still overwhelm even the gifted. Free technicians favor Orb bracers.
- **Tidekin Harborbands** generally use at least two distinct metal rings: upper valuation, lower security/obedience.
- **Specialized high-cost Imperial magical collars:** **Mirror Collar** records locally and cannot broadcast; footage is reviewed only after manual connection to an Orb. **Trance Collar** has high mana demand and suppresses higher reasoning if not resisted, making it unsuitable for most Kobold engineering work. The **Shock Collar** is medium-mana, deliberate/manual control normally, optionally checking exit from a *specific* Rainbow region; it cannot universally infer thoughts, weapon handling, secrets or intent, nor activate remotely from any arbitrary location. Their practical appearances override ordinary race-specific collars. Fine costs/durations/checks/thresholds are not approved.
- **Censure Collar / Censureband** is only a proposed name for the Shock Collar; **NOT APPROVED**.

**References:** `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md` (owning consolidated cultural dossier), `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `02_Races.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`. **Historical status at the time of this entry:** fine numeric mechanics, appearance artwork of magical collars, and the shock-collar name were then unapproved. The name was subsequently approved as **Coilsting Collar** above.

## 2. Canon approvals / revision history

### November 5, 2026 — Hero Gem wealth, consumables, shop buyback, and threat rewards (LATEST)

- **Explicit author approval:** `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` now owns the typical Hero equipment-wealth curve `W(L) = 3 + 3997 × ((L−1)/99)^2.3` (levels 1–100), targeting **3 Gems in equipment at level 1** and **4,000 Gems of currently owned equipment at level 100**, with the latter reflecting a **typical Hero who replaces equipment at every quality tier**, not an unusually thrifty Hero.
- **Approved spending and resale:** By level 100, approximately **30% of all Gems ever earned** is spent on consumables. Gem Shops buy back **items they originally sold** for **half their original purchase price**. Replacement losses are incorporated into the typical earnings budget, not compensated by a free reimbursement.
- **Reference model:** Quality milestones at Hero levels **10, 25, 50, 75, 100** are balancing assumptions, **not equipment-use prerequisites**. The representative portfolio values are **19 / 157 / 796 / 2,049 / 4,000 Gems**; four complete replacement resales create **~1,510.5 Gems of cumulative losses**. With `E(L) = (W(L) + R(L)) / 0.70`, the level-100 lifetime target is **~7,872 Gems earned**, **~2,362 Gems spent on consumables**, and **4,000 Gems currently in equipment**. Half-Gem arithmetic does not establish a fractional Gem denomination.
- **Gem reward multipliers explicitly match existing XP multipliers:** **Horde ×0.1, Minion ×0.4, Henchman ×1, Elite ×2**. A **Boss** grants a special Gem reward equivalent to **two levels of budgeted Gem progression**, including milestone replacement allowance where applicable; the existing Boss **XP** reward of exactly two level advances remains unchanged. **Do not adopt** the unapproved Elite ×3 / Minion ×0.5 XP suggestions from the immediately preceding discussion.
- **Reward calibration:** Base Gem rewards derive from the incremental lifetime earnings budget `E(L+1) − E(L)` and expected qualifying encounter/quest pacing. The exact encounter/quest allocation and fractional reward rounding remain unresolved. The two-level Boss Gem formula `E(L+2) − E(L)` is specified for **L+2 ≤100**; beyond-level-100 Gem progression is unresolved.
- **No changes:** Patron-specific eligibility, mundane quest limits, XP math, tier gates, shop catalogue item prices, five quality benchmarks, SP, and equipment usage requirements. Reference: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, indexed in `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.



### November 5, 2026 — Shimmer XP progression and enemy threat classes approved (LATEST)

- **Explicit user approval:** Hero XP required to advance from level **L** to **L + 1** is **`L × (L + 1)`**, as a per-level requirement, not cumulative lifetime XP. Example thresholds: level 1 **2 XP**, level 50 **2,550 XP**, level 100 **10,100 XP**. XP carries through level transitions.
- **Ordinary qualifying reward:** **`XP = 2 × encounter level × threat modifier`**. A defeated enemy uses its own level; a qualifying quest or nonviolent patron-approved achievement can use a GM-assigned equivalent challenge level. Round to nearest whole XP, minimum **1 XP for a qualifying encounter**. Patron-specific XP rules and the fact that mundane quests do not automatically award XP remain operative.
- **Threat modifiers and relative-level guidelines:** **Horde ×0.1** (enemy level ≤50% of Hero); **Minion ×0.4** (>50%–80%); **Henchman ×1** (>80%–110%); **Elite ×2** (>110%–140%); **Boss** (usually >140% or specially designated). These are **guidelines**; the GM assigns actual encounter class based on capabilities and context, not level ratio alone. The categories indicate swarmable trivial enemies, minor threats, near-peers, minibosses and prepared prolonged bosses respectively.
- **Boss exception:** A qualifying Boss grants the remaining XP to the next level **plus** the full XP for the following level, advancing the Hero **exactly two levels from the Hero's current level and progress**. At level 50 with no progress, the reward is **5,202 XP** and the Hero reaches level 52. Boss XP replaces, rather than multiplies, ordinary enemy XP.
- **Pacing:** One equal-level Henchman at level 1 yields the **2 XP** needed to level; at level 100, an equal-level Henchman gives **200 XP** against **10,100 XP**, so **51** encounters are needed from zero progress (approximately the requested 50).
- **Not approved by implication:** The previously floated anti-farming numerical restriction and any party XP division procedure remain unspecified; merely trivial/repetitive acts are not automatically patron-qualified. This approval does **not** change Shimmer attribute gains, Skill Point costs, rank prerequisites, or the goddesses' established reward criteria.
- **Owning file:** `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`; indexed in `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.


### November 5, 2026 — Style choice corrected to Direction 4 (LATEST)

- User explicitly corrected the selection: **Direction 4**, detailed ink-and-color fantasy, is the defining Mystaria style. Direction 5 is an archived alternative.
- Full guide now specifies delicate occasionally broken ink contours, sparse hatching, restrained translucent washes, subtle paper texture, economical expressive faces, practical clothing detail and grouped fur/feathers.
- Defining style reference is Race Image Origin — Direction 4, version 10. Original Race Image Origin v6 remains separately preserved.
- Adult female busty/voluptuous appearance, race-reference priority, deferred website work, collars and racial anatomy remain unchanged. Updated guide, index and repository instructions; historical selection below is superseded.

### November 5, 2026 — Direction 5 selection (SUPERSEDED by correction above); race references prioritized

- User selected the Japanese woodblock/modern anime hybrid as the defining style: rhythmic ink lines, designed negative space, layered flat scenery, stylized foliage/clouds, restrained separated pigments, selective textile patterns and subtle print texture, preserving animated characters and species anatomy.

### November 4, 2026 — Raw blue mana against wounded targets approved

- **Explicit user approval:** A Warlock's raw blue blast against a wounded target primarily causes **sensory overload and pain amplification**, with secondary **fractured bodily perception and disrupted coordination**.
- **Boundary:** Raw projection remains distinct from structured Sorcery; it does **not** freeze the target or become a splash of water. Approval is limited to this blue effect. Other optional proposals are not automatically canon.
- **Owning reference:** `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`; navigation summary in `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`. This adds an approved clarification where the dossier previously lacked a wounded-target raw-blue description; it does not replace the existing **Blue Hole — Shifting Depth**.
- **Preserved:** All existing Warlock skills, ranks, SP/MP costs, prerequisites, level gates, range, Handling Capacity, and external-source restrictions.
- **UNRESOLVED:** Exact duration, numerical penalties, resistance procedure, and damage formula for this effect. No new numerical mechanics are inferred.

- **Racial appearance:** Adult female Corvyn characteristically have particularly busty, voluptuous figures. This supersedes the earlier blanket slender-figure description while preserving their avian features, independent arms and separate back wings. No numerical stats or mechanics change.
- **Updated references:** `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, `02_Races.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, and `AGENTS.md`, plus this ledger. Existing collars, emblems and purple identity remain operative. The style specification is approved; `vol-peoples` version 6 remains unapproved.

They have **four solid-color eyes with no pupils**; eye color varies between individuals. Their long legs and imposing bodies make them **one of Mystaria's largest mortal races**.

### November 4, 2026 — Warlock direct MP Hand Capacity approved (LATEST)

- **Explicit approval:** Replace the old 1–3 spell-load system with direct temporary mana handling in **one hand**: **`max(1, floor(natural effective END / 5)) MP`**, i.e., **20% of natural effective END rounded down, minimum 1 MP**. For END 10/25/40/60/100/150/200, respective one-hand limits are **2/5/8/12/20/30/40 MP**.
- **Preserved restrictions:** A Warlock cannot generate or internally store mana, and retains the **30-second safe / 60-second absolute** hand-holding limits. The one-hand restriction remains; no implied doubled capacity, unlimited instantaneous absorption or bypass of the established throughput constraints. External mana sources and vessels are still essential.
- **Costs and other mechanics unchanged:** Existing 25 Manacraft ranks and **125 SP** mastery, all activation/upkeep/manifestation fees, prerequisites, range formula, Intelligence contest, and staging requirements continue unchanged. Questions about **same-action harvesting**, direct external payment of oversized fees, advanced effect scales and lifetimes remain unresolved; this capacity ruling does not settle them.
- **Supersedes:** The former **`max(1, ceil(natural effective END / 10)) MP` per spell-load** calculation, its 1–3 held-loads chart, and the missing category thresholds. Owning reference: `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`; cross-reference: `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.

### November 4, 2026 — Mindprism / Mnemonic Arts / Living Reliquaries approved (LATEST)

- **Approved technology and vocabulary:** Retinal **Mindprism** uses specially calibrated **Cipherlight** for the **Mnemonic Arts**. Approved terms: **Echo, Echo Orb, Everseal, Mindwright, Deepprint, Echoing, Mindcut, Echo Vault, Memory Bond, Dreamcasting, Dreamcaster, Dreamhouse, Skillprint, Living Reliquary, Hollow Reliquary**. Ordinary direct Cipherlight beams remain dangerously confusing.
- **Recording/medical ruling:** Copying a memory consumes **extravagant amounts of actual mana**, is expensive and often impractical; a basic recording has a **firearm-purchase-level relative price**, not a fixed Gem amount. Individual calibration and extreme neurological strain apply. Broad Deepprints can take days or years. Magic cannot heal this particular neurological injury: time is required, and severe Mindcuts can leave permanent damage. **Mindcut erases the original source memories** but can be much faster; it may be used to induce amnesia.
- **Law of Returning Echoes:** Recorded memories cannot be destroyed by mortal technology. All copies remain tied to the original source. Unsealed Echoes return to the **living** original owner; a continuously maintained **Everseal** using some actual mana prevents return. If every surviving implanted copy is lost with its recipients, the memory returns to the still-living owner. **Death of the original owner ENDS automatic return.** Returned memory does not cure brain damage; divine interventions retain their own laws.
- **Skillprints and Shimmer:** An advanced **Skillprint** can bestow an **actual Shimmer skill counted as purchased at no SP cost**. Normal level gates, chains and racial/physical/magical prerequisites remain controlling; no innate mana or Shimmer for non-Heroes. **~5 SP worth of skills per month** is a modest safety benchmark, with neurological harm risk beyond it. Such Skillprints are rare, usually medium- or high-level Hero treasures. No normal SP purchase costs, rank names or unrelated Crafts are modified.
- **Culture:** **Dreamcasting** streams actual subjective experience **in real time**, as distinct from uploading a previously recorded Echo. **Dreamhouses** and professional Dreamcasters are a major entertainment industry. **Echo Vaults** use maintained Echo Orbs, **Memory Bonds** pledge memory collateral. **Living Reliquaries** are enslaved intelligent living archives of foreign memories, while **Hollow Reliquaries** have their own histories forcibly Mindcut away; neither has unlimited capacity or loses personhood.
- **Canon boundaries:** This is a new technology, not a new Shimmer purchase table, Craft skill tree, Warlock soul vessel, or divine memory rule. The detailed controlling document is `19_The_Mnemonic_Arts_and_Echo_Economy.md`. Supporting references: `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`, `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`.
- **Open:** Exact MP and Gem prices, recording/upload timing formulae, Everseal mana and maintenance rates, host resistance/return timing, postmortem final Echo disposition, and staged Skillprint uploads. These are not settled by the approval.


### November 4, 2026 — Cipherlight, Coilnet Rainbows, language, and Mirrorwake (LATEST)

- **Approved name:** **Cipherlight** is the encoded, information-bearing optical medium of Kobold Orbcraft and Mirrorcraft; computational Orbs and Magic Mirrors encode/interpret it. **Lighthouses** act as beam relays. Reflective surfaces permit transmissions through water, including submerged and underground pipelines, potentially offering greater route security than exposed relays.
- **Approved coverage phenomenon:** An active Coilnet's coverage region is its **Rainbow**, visibly indicated by a subtle aurora-like upper-atmospheric shimmer; heavy traffic makes it stronger and more obvious. Even small independent networks (e.g. near a Drakyn lair) can attract interest. A new Rainbow can tip off guards to possible covert transmissions/foreign influence but does not prove a crime, pinpoint senders, or decode traffic.
- **Approved direct beam hazard:** Staring directly into Cipherlight produces **extreme confusion**. Numerical exposure and resistance mechanics were not specified.
- **Approved language:** The user approved the Kobold and regional sayings “You're speaking Cipherlight,” “Don't stare into the beam,” “Bright Rainbow!”, “Lost in the Rainbow,” “There's light beneath the water,” “All beam, no message,” “Under the same Rainbow,” “The Rainbow is burning,” “Beyond the Rainbow,” and “a beautiful headache.” These supplement rather than supersede older idioms. See `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`.
- **Approved regional reception:** Reactions vary among Imperial residents, Canopy populations, Vren, Duskpaws, Tidekin and remote Drakyn lairs. The regional security consequences appear in `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`.
- **Approved Tidekin interaction:** **Mirrorwake detects Cipherlight through water exceptionally well** and retains strong, significant impressions of it. High-intensity traffic can overwhelm and obscure other water memories. Deliberate Cipherlight use can conceal evidence, and **particularly sophisticated interference can create false Mirrorwake evidence**. Detection does not automatically decrypt Coilnet communications; forging evidence is not automatically successful. This expands an environmental interaction only; **Mirrorwake ranks, SP costs and existing techniques are unchanged**. Owning reference `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`.
- **Unresolved:** Precise optical coding, speed, range, atmospheric signal strength, direct-beam confusion duration, Mirrorwake forgery requirements and detection/contest tests. Do not invent quantitative standards.
- **Updated files:** `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`, `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md`, and this changelog.


### November 4, 2026 — Warlock Manacraft skill-tree replacement and MP costs approved

- **Explicit user decision:** Replace all five formerly approved Warlock skill trees with the recovered *Warlock Skills System, Revised* manuscript's **25 ranks**, without adopting its proposed progressive SP schedule. **All ranks cost 5 SP each**, five ranks per tree, **125 SP mastery**; existing Hero level gates I/1, II/10, III/25, IV/50, V/80 and rank prerequisites remain. Base Manabending remains free and Warlocks cannot generate or internally store mana.
- **New canonical progression:** Mana Reach — Unseen Hand, Crooked Thread, Binding Thread, Walk the Current, Ley Carving; Mana Power — Focused Expression, Flowing Expression, Sustained Expression, Pressure Geometry, Mana Singularity; Mana Harvest — Afterglow, Ambient Harvest, Hollow Radius, Fundamental Conversion, Soul Reaping; Mana Imbuement — Reservoir Seal, Triggered Release, Conditional Enchantment, Spell Imprint, Soulwork; Mana Dominion — Deflection, Spell Theft, Soul Hush, Sovereign Thread, Null Dominion.
- **Mana Singularity (V):** The five holes are **Red Furnace Heart; Blue Shifting Depth; Green Genesis Bloom; White Immutable Crown; Black Final Silence**. These are temporary energy concentrations, not literal infinite-mass black holes.
- **Base Manabending replaced:** Reach = `2 + 2 × effective INT` meters; safe temporary hand holding 30 sec, hard limit 60 sec. **Historical and superseded:** this original version handled `max(1, ceil(natural effective END / 10)) MP` per reference spell-load and held 1–3 loads; the subsequent November 4 direct-hand-capacity ruling above replaces this with **`max(1, floor(natural effective END / 5)) MP` in one hand**. The recovered absorption contest is `min(100%, Warlock INT / (2 × Caster INT) × 100%)`, with current Handling Capacity still limiting each interaction.
- **User approved MP costs in the preceding exchange:** Full rank-by-rank **activation, upkeep, released-energy and conditional fees** are now operative in `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`, including **Mana Singularity 12 MP activation + minimum 20 MP manifestation energy; 4 MP/round + minimum 10 MP released per round**; **Ley Carving 12 MP activation, 6 MP/minute during alteration and at least 50 extra MP for a lasting regional alteration**; **Soul Reaping 20 MP activation + 5 MP/round**; **Soulwork 15 MP per soul vessel / 10 MP per construct, plus 5 MP/day or 4 MP/day respectively**; **Null Dominion 15 MP + 6 MP/round**. For the remaining 20 ranks, consult the subject dossier; do not infer old rank costs for different powers.
- **Superseded:** Former technique trees, notably Thousand Invisible Hands, Grand Expression, Great Reclamation, Living Arsenal and Absolute Reversal, and their rank-specific MP schedules; the recovered author's proposed **1/3/5/7/9 SP** schedule; approximate 2-meter basic Manabending range and END-sized hand reservoir where inconsistent. Prior **November 3 Warlock approval remains historically true**, not operative for conflicting techniques.
- **Still unresolved:** the former 1–3 spell-load thresholds were **RESOLVED** by direct MP Hand Capacity; higher-rank Imbuement seal/imprint lifetime, exact field radius/limits, same-action harvesting to pay activation, and payment of high activation fees directly from external sources without bypassing active handling remain unresolved. Do not make numerical claims absent explicit approval.
- Updated the owning `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`, Canon Index and Canon Changelog copies in the downloadable November 4 update.

### November 4, 2026 — Linguistic expansion recovered and fully approved (current ruling)

- The user recovered the original full linguistic proposal (`Pasted text.txt`) and the subsequent revised proposal (`Pasted text (2).txt`). The user explicitly clarified that **the entire original proposal and all unchanged material are canon**, subject to **later revisions taking precedence**. Earlier unrecovered-source warnings in the November 4 draft are superseded.
- Approved `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` as the owning dossier for Roadspeech, Imperial Coil Speech, Canopy Cant, Red Cant, race-related speaking tendencies, insults, compliments, curses, phrase meanings and misuse, slang, social etiquette, slaveholder/enslaved speech, and illustrative dialogue. These are cultural tendencies, not mandatory racial accents.
- **Current Naga wording:** “Mind your tail.” and “A monarch needn't hiss.” Supersede “Mind your coils.” and “A crown needn't hiss.”
- **Current Kobold wording:** “Son of a glitch!”; “Almost done.”; “Bright as a first spark.”; “Are your scales just for show?” These supersede “Cracked lattice!”, “One more adjustment.”, “That's a First Spark”, and “You were never worth the imprint.” Added “Broken mirrors.” (bad luck / “Why me?”). **“Oops!”** is an imminent-catastrophe warning even when the speaker caused no error. Kobold imagery emphasizes illumination, Mirrors, Orbs and discovery over mechanical-part sayings.

- **Current Drider wording:** “You enjoy the sentence more than the verdict.” Supersedes the earlier “...more than the justice.”

- **Speaking in Colors:** Explicitly approved, definitive casual dialogue convention. The five magical energies are typically named by color (“shape the white,” “out of black”); the term **mana** remains standard in precise, academic, technical, mechanical, and Shimmer contexts. The magic mechanics themselves are unchanged.
- Added approved magical praise/insults/slang for the represented peoples. Treat scenes as examples of linguistic style, not new mechanical permissions.

The author's later explicit ruling removed that unidentified purchase. The current approved ledger is **25 alterations** and **90/60 SP**, preserving all named effects.

## 3. Superseded concepts not to revive silently

| Old assumption | Controlling later rule |
|---|---|
| Dragons called Vyrathi or similar names | **Drakyn** |
| Drakyn naturally regenerate mana at rest | **0 natural MP, 0 rest regeneration; must consume mana-bearing creatures** |
| Drakyn elemental identity purely inherited by genetics | **Element set by egg incubation environment** |
| Drakyn spell-gift mark only teaches concepts, not an Incantation | **May grant the purchased-knowledge effect directly, with recipient Soul Strain / SP buyoff** |
| Jester inversion, Recompense and Final Inversion | **Physical Stitch / Regalia six-discipline system** |
| Regalia merely semisentient or mute | **Fully sentient, speaking, independently motivated** |
| Hollow Jesters removed with inversion system | **Hollow Jesters still exist as rare narrative outcome** |
| Moontouched categorically unable to become Jesters | **Any race capable of Donning can bond with Regalia** |
| Warlock old per-rank MP costs | **The old techniques and their MP fees were superseded by the November 4 replacement; new approved costs govern** |
| Warlock rank effects absent | **Current recovered replacement 25 ranks and approved MP fees reside in `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`** |
| Centaurs have initial DEX 5, END 10, INT 8 | **DEX 3, END 8, INT 6** |
| Celestite a mundane high-tier metal or extra numerical upgrade | **Unique divine relic outside regular five quality tiers** |
| Proposed 8-tree Anvilcant treated as already approved | **Not canon until explicitly approved** |
| Older Shimmer progression of 1 stat point per level | **Later 2 per level +1 all every fifth level** |

6. **Warlock Imbuement:** the former Living Arsenal is superseded. Rank I Reservoir Seal lasts one hour; Rank IV imprint and Rank V Soulwork lifespan remains unquantified aside from the approved daily upkeep fees.

9. **Firstshaped-specific racial rarity:** especially common in Centaurs/Drakyn vs older generic ~1 in 50.
10. **Other racial Craft trees:** complete, individually approved rank descriptions were not supplied; do not invent them in `08_Other_Crafts.md`.

## 5. Maintaining this library

1. When a new decision is explicitly canonized, update its owning dossier and **this changelog**; update `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md` if precedence or file boundaries change.
2. Preserve old approved details when revision scope is limited, but label superseded sections clearly rather than leaving contradictory alternatives as coequal rules.
3. When an ability remains a **proposal**, place it under a heading that explicitly says so and keep it separate from the operative rules.
4. Do not replace original project Files merely by generating a download in chat. Upload/replace the new files within the Mystaria Lore project's **Files section**.
5. In a different ChatGPT project conversation, open `00_Mystaria_Canon_Index.md` and the relevant subject files before adjudicating numerical mechanics.
